Result: Gemini-2.5-Flash和LLaMA-3.3-70B-Instruct在乌克兰语上性能下降，DeepSeek-V3保持稳定。人类和AI评估者在同理心和自然度上弱一致，但在文化根基上存在显著分歧。

Conclusion: 生成乌克兰语文本不等于生成乌克兰语情感支持。研究有助于未来开发更具文化针对性的基准设计，并强调以人为中心的评估的重要性。

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly deployed in emotional-support contexts and crisis-related situations. Nevertheless, their cross-lingual abilities in these circumstances remain underexplored. Existing benchmarks emphasize multilingual performance but rarely examine crisis-related empathy and cultural grounding in low-to-mid-resource languages. We introduce SPLIT, a 500-prompt benchmark designed to evaluate LLM consistency in generating emotionally grounded responses across five categories: Stress, Panic, Loneliness, Internal Displacement, and Tension. We evaluate three technically diverse LLMs across three dimensions: Empathetic Accuracy, Linguistic Naturalness, and Contextual & Cultural Grounding. The framework aims to assess and compare the quality of LLM responses in both English and Ukrainian languages, as well as to explore the reliability of the LLM-as-a-jury paradigm. Our findings reveal that Gemini-2.5-Flash and LLaMA-3.3-70B-Instruct degrade when transitioning to Ukrainian, while DeepSeek-V3 remains comparatively stable within our benchmark. We additionally find that human and AI evaluators agree weakly on empathy and naturalness but diverge on cultural grounding. We further argue that producing Ukrainian text is not equivalent to producing Ukrainian emotional support. Our findings may assist in the future development of more culturally tailored benchmark designs, as well as encourage a stronger emphasis on human-centered evaluation.

</details>


### [40] [HaloGuard 1.0: An Open Weights Constitutional Classifier for Multilingual AI Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02079)
*Navaneeth Sangameswaran,Preetham S,Ashmiya Lenin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HaloGuard 1.0 是一种开源权重的宪法分类器输入安全模型，在英语和多语言提示安全基准上以十分之一于领先模型的尺寸达到SOTA性能，采用46条政策/2940个子类别的自然语言宪法驱动合成数据生成，通过一对一配对反事实、双层无害设计及46种语言平衡多语言实现，0.8B模型平均F1达90.9，4B模型达92.1，持续对抗性红队协议强化防护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放安全防护模型体积庞大，且难以在保持低误报率的同时有效检测多语言及边界案例中的有害输入，需要一种更小、更高效且更精确的输入安全方案。

Method: 提出HaloGuard架构：1) 46条政策+2940子类别的自然语言宪法驱动合成数据生成；2) 一对一配对反事实（固定主题和词汇，翻转意图）；3) 双层无害设计分别针对边界和基线假阳性；4) 46种语言的平衡多语言实现（视语言为表面形式而非对抗信号）；5) 持续在线的对抗性红队协议。

Result: HaloGuard 1.0-0.8B：7个提示安全基准平均F1 90.9，FPR 4.3，FNR 9.5，超越基线模型（包括27B参数模型）。HaloGuard 1.0-4B：平均F1 92.1，FPR 3.5，将额外容量用于提高精确率而非召回率。剩余失败中大部分为基准误标。

Conclusion: HaloGuard 1.0以十分之一的模型尺寸实现了领先的输入安全性能，验证了宪法分类器范式的有效性，开源权重可促进安全AI社区发展。

Abstract: We present HaloGuard 1.0, an open-weights implementation of the constitutional-classifier paradigm for input safety. It achieves state-of-the-art performance on English and multilingual prompt-safety benchmarks at roughly one-tenth the model size of current leading open guard models. The safety constitution is the organising structure of the corpus: a natural-language constitution of 46 policies and 2,940 subcategories drives synthetic data generation, with exhaustive one-to-one paired counterfactuals that hold topic and vocabulary fixed while flipping intent, a two-tier harmless design that separately targets boundary and baseline false positives (FPs), and balanced multilingual materialisation across 46 languages that treats language as a surface form appearing on both sides of the boundary rather than as an adversarial signal. Across seven prompt-safety benchmarks, HaloGuard 1.0-0.8B attains the best average F1 (90.9) of any open guard we evaluate, outperforming baselines up to 27B parameters (over 30 times larger) while holding false-positive rate (FPR) to 4.3 and false-negative rate (FNR) to 9.5. The HaloGuard 1.0-4B variant reaches average F1 of 92.1 and FPR of 3.5, spending its extra capacity on precision rather than recall. A structured adjudication of the remaining failures indicates that most apparent missed-harm cases are benchmark mislabels rather than genuine model misses. An always-on adversarial red-teaming protocol continuously hardens the guard against both content-level and agentic attacks. We release the models as open weights.

</details>


### [41] [Unlocking Speech-Text Compositional Powers: Instruction-Following Speech Language Models without Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02214)
*Congrui Du,Yang Zhang,Kaizhi Qian,Shiyu Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SpeechCombine是一种无需指令微调即可训练语音语言模型的方法，仅通过30k小时数据的单轮语音预训练，结合文本LLM的权重差异，就能将文本LLM的知识和能力迁移到语音领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音语言模型的指令微调远比文本LLM困难，因为需要学习新模态和大量语音特定指令；现有方法通过合成大规模语音预训练和指令微调数据集来复制文本LLM训练范式，但语音序列远长于文本序列，导致难以扩展。

Method: 从文本LLM基础模型出发，对语音话语进行连续预训练获得语音适配模型，然后将其权重与指令微调版本和基础版本文本LLM之间的权重差值直接合并。整个过程无需指令微调，仅需单轮30k小时数据的语音预训练。

Result: 这种简单的权重合并策略不仅能保留原始文本LLM的知识和能力，还能有效将其迁移到语音领域。

Conclusion: 为SLM训练提供了新方向，避免了对大规模语音数据的依赖。

Abstract: Instruction tuning for speech language models (SLMs) is substantially more challenging than for text-based large language models (LLMs), as it requires learning a new modality and a wide range of speech-specific instructions in addition to those supported by text LLMs. Existing SLM training approaches largely replicate the text LLM training paradigm by synthesizing large-scale speech pre-training and instruction-tuning datasets. However, this strategy is difficult to scale, since speech sequences are significantly longer than text sequences. In this paper, we propose SpeechCombine, an instruction-following speech language model trained without any instruction tuning, using only a single round of speech pre-training on 30k hours of data. Starting from a text LLM base model, we perform continuous pre-training on speech utterances to obtain a speech-adapted model, and then directly combine its weights with the weight difference between the instruction-tuned and base versions of the text LLM. Our results show that this simple combination strategy not only preserves the knowledge and capabilities of the original text LLM, but also effectively transfers them to the speech domain. These findings suggest a new direction for SLM training that avoids reliance on massive speech data.

</details>


### [42] [Challenges and Recommendations for LLMs-as-a-Judge in Multilingual Settings and Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02235)
*A. Seza Doğruöz,Xixian Liao,Verena Blaschke,Jakob Prange,Senyu Li,David Ifeoluwa Adelani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文调查了LLM-as-a-Judge评估范式在多语言和低资源场景下的应用现状。在650篇提及该范式的论文中，仅33篇关注低资源或多语言设置。研究发现存在评估结果不一致、过度信任LLM判断、以及依赖单一评判模型等问题，并提出了相应建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge已成为许多自然语言生成任务的主流评估范式，但主要局限于英语场景。随着该范式向多语言和低资源语言扩展，由于LLM在这些语言上能力有限且缺乏足够的人工验证，需要系统性地审视当前实践中存在的问题。

Method: 对ACL Anthology中提及LLM-as-a-Judge的650篇论文进行筛选，聚焦于关注多语言和低资源设置的33篇论文，进行深入分析和总结。

Result: 仅33篇（约5%）论文关注低资源或多语言设置；这些论文存在评估结果不一致、过度信任LLM在多语言设置中的判断、以及广泛依赖单一评判模型等问题。

Conclusion: 当前LLM-as-a-Judge在多语言和低资源语言场景下的应用存在显著问题，作者提出了相关建议以帮助NLP社区更合理地使用该评估范式。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge has become the dominant evaluation paradigm for many natural language generation tasks, due to shortcomings of conventional metrics and high correlations with human judgment, albeit mostly in English. There are now attempts to extend LLM-as-a-Judge to multilingual settings including low-resource languages. However, LLMs have limited proficiency in low-resource languages, and there is often no adequate human validation in these settings. To highlight the scope of the problem and current practices, we explore the use of LLM-as-a-Judge evaluators in ACL Anthology papers focusing on multilingual settings and low-resource languages across a diverse set of tasks. Out of 650 papers mentioning LLM-as-a-judge, only 33 of them focus on low-resource or multilingual settings. Our in-depth analysis of these papers indicates inconsistent evaluation outcomes, a tendency to overtrust LLM judgments in multilingual settings, and the widespread reliance on a single judge model per study. To help the NLP community further, we conclude with recommendations about how to use LLM-as-a-Judge in multilingual and low-resource settings.

</details>


### [43] [BamiBERT: A New BERT-based Language Model for Vietnamese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02259)
*Dat Quoc Nguyen,Thinh Pham,Chi Tran,Linh The Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BamiBERT是专为越南语设计的BERT变体预训练语言模型，在129GB语料上训练20个epoch，支持最长2048 token的上下文，无需外部分词，在8个越南语基准测试的15项指标中取得11项最优和3项次优，达到base规模越南语编码器的新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决PhoBERT作为当前越南语事实标准文本编码器存在的关键局限性，包括上下文长度限制和对外部分词工具的依赖。

Method: 从零开始在129GB通用域越南语文本语料上训练20个epoch，支持2048 token的扩展上下文长度，直接对原始输入操作无需外部分词。

Result: 在8个越南语基准测试的15项指标中，获得11项最优和3项次优，在base规模越南语编码器中达到新SOTA，展现了强跨领域泛化能力。

Conclusion: BamiBERT通过更大规模训练数据、更长上下文支持以及免分词的直接输入方式，有效克服了PhoBERT的局限性，在越南语自然语言处理任务上取得了显著性能提升。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce BamiBERT, a new BERT-based pre-trained language model for Vietnamese that addresses key limitations of PhoBERT -- the current de facto Vietnamese text encoder. Trained from scratch on a 129GB corpus of general-domain Vietnamese text for 20 epochs, BamiBERT supports an extended context length of up to 2048 tokens and operates directly on raw input, eliminating the need for external word segmentation. Across 8 Vietnamese benchmarks, it achieves the best score on 11 of 15 metrics and the second-best on 3 others, setting a new state of the art among "base"-sized Vietnamese encoders and demonstrating strong cross-domain generalization. We release BamiBERT at: https://huggingface.co/Qualcomm-AI-Research/BamiBERT

</details>


### [44] [CheckRLM: Effective Knowledge-Thought Coherence Checking in Retrieval-Augmented Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02262)
*Dingling Xu,Ruobing Wang,Qingfei Zhao,Yukun Yan,Zhichun Wang,Daren Zha,Shi Yu,Zhenghao Liu,Shuo Wang,Xu Han,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CheckRLM是一个通过检索增强生成（RAG）在推理过程中及时检测并纠正事实错误的框架，有效提升推理语言模型在知识密集型任务中的可靠性，并降低长程推理中的错误累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理语言模型（RLMs）在复杂任务中通过扩展推理链提升了性能，但推理链中容易包含事实错误，尤其是在知识密集型任务中，这一问题亟待解决。

Method: CheckRLM框架从推理链中提取事实性声明，识别并定位推理过程中的知识不一致性；一旦检测到错误，通过利用外部知识进行最小成本的精确修正，确保推理链与正确知识之间的连贯性。

Result: 大量实验表明，CheckRLM显著优于现有基线方法，能够有效缓解长程推理中的错误累积，且成本更低。

Conclusion: CheckRLM通过RAG机制实现了对推理链事实错误的及时检查与精确修正，提升了推理过程的可靠性，在知识密集型任务中展现出优越性能。

Abstract: Reasoning Language Models (RLMs) have significantly improved performance on complex tasks by extending the reasoning chain. However, these chains are prone to containing factual errors, particularly in knowledge-intensive tasks. To address this issue, we propose CheckRLM, a framework that improves the reliability of the reasoning process through Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) by timely checking and correcting factual errors. Specifically, CheckRLM extracts factual claims from the reasoning chain to identify and localize subtle knowledge inconsistencies during inference. Upon detection of errors, a refinement mechanism performs minimal-cost yet precise corrections by leveraging external knowledge, ensuring coherence between the reasoning chain and correct knowledge. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CheckRLM substantially outperforms existing baselines, exhibiting a strong capability to mitigate error accumulation in long-horizon reasoning with lower costs. The code and data are available at https://github.com/AI9Stars/CheckRLM.

</details>


### [45] [On the Role of Directionality in Structural Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02307)
*Zichao Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于CCG有向类型（确定性CKY+单线性解码器，仅3万可学习参数）替代原有AM代数无方向操作，在相同BERT-base编码器下将LF精确匹配率从70.8%提升至75.9%，方向性改进主要提升位置偏移类别（+29.9pp），而递归深度类别则受益于更强的编码器（DeBERTa-v3-large达90.7%），表明方向性表示将瓶颈从符号层转移至神经层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLOG测试类别明确涉及方向性区分（修饰语位置偏移、论元提取位置），但先前最先进的AM-Parser使用的AM代数操作不编码方向，导致方向性信息缺失成为性能瓶颈。

Method: 将符号后端重新设计为基于CCG有向类型，采用确定性CKY算法+单线性解码器，仅含3万个可学习参数；在相同BERT-base编码器下与AM-Parser对比，并进一步替换为DeBERTa-v3-large编码器分析不同类别的增益来源。

Result: 基于BERT-base的CCG系统LF精确匹配率达75.9±6.4%，优于AM-Parser的70.8±4.3%；在全部5个位置偏移类别上超越AM-Parser（+29.9pp），而在全部6个递归深度类别上AM-Parser更优；替换DeBERTa-v3-large编码器后达90.7±4.9%，递归深度类别增益最大。

Conclusion: 方向性表示将性能瓶颈从符号层（AM-Parser的0%类别上限）转移至神经层，神经层可通过编码器升级持续改进，为语义解析中符号与神经组件的分工提供了新视角。

Abstract: Several SLOG test categories explicitly involve directional distinctions (modifier position shifts, argument extraction positions), yet AM-Parser, the previous SOTA, uses an AM algebra whose operations do not encode direction. We redesign the symbolic backend around CCG directed types (deterministic CKY + single linear decoder, 30K learnable parameters). Under the same BERT-base encoder, the system achieves 75.9$\pm$6.4% LF exact match, surpassing AM-Parser (70.8$\pm$4.3%). Per SLOG's own category groupings, gains are highly directional: the CCG system outperforms AM-Parser on all 5 position-shift categories (+29.9pp), while AM-Parser outperforms on all 6 recursive-depth categories. Replacing the encoder with DeBERTa-v3-large yields 90.7$\pm$4.9%, with the largest encoder gains in recursive-depth categories, complementary to directionality's gains. Directional representations shift the bottleneck from the symbolic layer (AM-Parser's 0% category ceiling) to the neural layer, which improves with encoder upgrades.

</details>


### [46] [World Wide Models: Literary Tools for Cultural AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02369)
*Nina Begus*

Main category: cs.CL

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM带来了大规模、自动化且单语的新型文化相遇，而现有AI缺乏文化素养，需要文学学科的工具来弥补这一缺陷。

Method: 提出了一个分层框架，连接批判理论与结构性单语主义的当前辩论，并将世界文学方法（宏观结构、流通、不可译性）应用于全球AI文本性分析。

Result: 形成了一种面向更细致的文本模型和多元AI解释的分析框架，强调了文学与AI发展的自然交集。

Conclusion: 文学学科的工具对于构建文化素养的AI不可或缺，世界文学方法为应对AI的全球文本性和单语主义问题提供了新路径。

Abstract: LLMs stage a new form of cultural encounter that is massive, automated, and monolingual. Literary disciplines have always negotiated cultural struggles with comparative reading of literature, narratological and poetic analysis, critical theory, world literature, and translation. These tools have now become indispensable for building culturally literate AI. The essay develops a layered framework toward more nuanced textual models and pluralistic interpretations of AI, emphasizing the natural intersections of literature and AI development, connecting current debates in critical theory with structural monolingualism, and suggesting a new application of world literature approaches to address global AI textuality through macrostructure, circulation, and untranslatability.

</details>

*Lourdes Moreno,Paloma Martínez,Marco Antonio Sanchez-Escudero,Miguel Domínguez-Gómez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文描述了HULAT2-UC3M团队在MER-TRANS 2026西班牙语Easy-to-Read翻译任务中的参与情况。提交了三个全自动西班牙语运行系统：RUN1和RUN2采用基于LangGraph的多智能体工作流（Gemini 2.5 Flash + RigoChat-7B-v2），包含并行生成、内部质量信号、ECA路由、受控编辑和可追溯决策；RUN1为基础版本，RUN2增加了基于词汇表和资源的词汇支持层。RUN3是基于RigoChat的生成-评估-再生成基线（提示工程+LoRA适配）。官方榜单显示，RUN1以SARI 44.0543分排名最佳，RUN2为43.1049，RUN3为38.5136。结果表明信号引导的多智能体路由优于线性再生成基线，且添加词汇支持并未自动提升基于参考的评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索多智能体工作流在Easy-to-Read翻译任务中的有效性，比较信号引导的多智能体路由与线性再生成基线的性能差异，并评估词汇支持层对翻译质量的实际影响。

Method: 基于LangGraph构建多智能体工作流，集成Gemini 2.5 Flash和RigoChat-7B-v2两个模型，采用并行生成策略、内部质量信号、事件-条件-动作（ECA）路由机制、受控编辑和可追溯决策。RUN2额外增加基于词汇表和词汇资源的词汇支持层。RUN3基线采用生成-评估-再生成循环，结合提示工程和LoRA适配。

Result: 官方评测中RUN1以SARI 44.0543分最优，RUN2为43.1049，RUN3为38.5136。开发阶段还检验了语义忠实度、可读性、词汇简洁性、句法清晰度和事实一致性等内部信号。

Conclusion: 信号引导的多智能体路由在本任务设置中显著优于线性再生成基线；添加词汇支持层并未自动提升基于参考的评分；需要进一步的句子级和文档级分析来评估可读性、事实一致性和用户导向的充分性。

Abstract: This paper describes the participation of HULAT2-UC3M in the Spanish track of MER-TRANS 2026, a shared task on multilingual Easy-to-Read translation. Three fully automatic Spanish runs were submitted. RUN1 and RUN2 used a LangGraph-based multi-agent workflow combining Gemini 2.5 Flash and RigoChat-7B-v2, parallel generation strategies, internal quality signals, Event-Condition-Action routing, controlled editing and traceable decisions. RUN1 used the base workflow, while RUN2 activated an additional lexical-support layer based on a glossary and lexical resources. RUN3 was a RigoChat-based generate-evaluate-regenerate baseline with prompt engineering and LoRA-based adaptation. The official leaderboard reports BLEU-Orig, BLEU-Gold, SARI and BERTScore. During development, additional internal signals were also inspected, including semantic fidelity, readability, lexical simplicity, syntactic clarity and factual consistency. According to official SARI, RUN1 was the best HULAT2 run, with 44.0543 points, followed by RUN2 with 43.1049 and RUN3 with 38.5136. These results indicate that, in this task setting, signal-guided multi-agent routing outperformed the linear regeneration baseline. They also show that adding lexical support did not automatically improve reference-based scores. Further segment-level and document-level analysis are required to assess readability, factual consistency and user-oriented adequacy.

</details>


### [48] [Know Your Source: A Public Knowledge Store for Media Background Checks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02383)
*Benjamin Nichols,Michael Schlichtkrull,Nedjma Ousidhoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MEDIAREF，一个公开可用的网络文档知识库，用于可重复、低成本的媒体背景检查（MBC）生成评估，解决现有RAG事实核查中依赖昂贵专有搜索API的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RAG的自动化事实核查系统通常假设检索到的证据可靠，但现实中的信息可能相互矛盾、过时或来自不可靠来源。虽然近期工作通过媒体背景检查（MBC）评估信息来源可信度来解决这一问题，但生成MBC依赖昂贵的专有搜索API，限制了可重复性。

Method: 构建MEDIAREF，一个覆盖200个媒体来源的网络文档知识库；提出可重复的构建与更新方法；评估多种主流LLM在MBC生成任务上的表现。

Result: MEDIAREF能够支持更高质量的MBC生成，通过自动评估和定性评估均得到验证。

Conclusion: MEDIAREF作为公开资源，提供了可重复、低成本的方式来评估和生成媒体背景检查，有望推动源批判推理在事实核查中的广泛应用。

Abstract: LLM-based retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is increasingly used for automated fact-checking (AFC) and related tasks. By grounding LLM outputs in retrieved evidence, RAG-based systems provide transparent justifications while allowing external information to be updated independently of the underlying model. However, existing approaches often assume retrieved evidence is reliable, although real-world information may be conflicting, outdated, and can originate from unreliable or biased sources. Recent work on *source-critical reasoning* addresses this challenge through media background checks (MBCs) (Schlichtkrull, 2024), which assess the credibility of evidence sources to support downstream fact verification. However, generating MBCs relies on costly proprietary search APIs, limiting reproducibility. To mitigate this issue, we introduce MEDIAREF, a publicly available knowledge store of web-sourced documents that enables reproducible, low-cost evaluation of MBC generation across 200 media sources. We describe a reproducible methodology for constructing and updating the collection, assess widely used LLMs on the MBC generation task, and demonstrate that MEDIAREF supports higher-quality MBC generation through both automatic and qualitative evaluation.

</details>


### [49] [The Future of NLP may not be at NLP Conferences: Scholarly Migration Patterns in Natural Language Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02416)
*David Jurgens*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NLP研究正从传统ACL等核心会议向通用ML venue迁移，LLM时代后，资深作者在ACL主会份额下降19.2pp，在Findings track增加14.8pp，在通用ML venue增加8.6pp；新作者主要发表在ACL的比例从84%降至74%，而通用ML venue从5%升至21%。因果推断显示通用ML venue提供显著引用溢价，推动这一迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM时代下NLP研究的学科重心是否正在从传统NLP核心会议（如ACL）向通用机器学习会议迁移。

Method: 分析2010-2026年NLP论文数据，分别考察资深作者和新作者的发文分布变化，并运用因果推断技术估计不同venue类型的引用优势。

Result: (1) 资深作者在*ACL主会份额下降19.2pp，Findings track增加14.8pp，通用ML venue上升8.6pp；(2) 新作者以ACL为主的比例从84%降至74%，以通用ML为主的比例从5%升至21%；(3) 通用ML venue存在显著的引用溢价。

Conclusion: NLP研究的出版重心正在显著从ACL等传统NLP会议向通用ML venue转移，引用优势是驱动这一迁移的重要因素。

Abstract: Natural Language Processing (NLP) has traditionally been published in its core disciplinary venues like ACL. However, advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) has led to a blurring of the disciplinary lines between NLP and general Machine Learning (ML), with authors regularly publishing in venues from both fields. Here, we ask whether the disciplinary center of gravity is shifting. Using NLP research published from 2010 to 2026 and studies of both established and new authors, we find that a migration is taking place. First, comparing the pre- and post-LLM eras, established authors lost 19.2pp of share at flagship *ACL main-conference tracks while gaining 14.8pp in the newer Findings tracks, and general ML venues rose 8.6pp, even when adjusting for parallel growth in the fields. Second, among newer authors who debut with at least three first-author NLP-topic papers, the share whose work appears mostly at *ACL venues fell from 84% (2019) to 74% (2024), while the share appearing mostly at general ML venues rose from 5% to 21%. Using causal inference techniques, we estimate that these general ML venues confer a significant citation premium, which influences venue selection. Together, these results point to a significant shift in where NLP research is published.

</details>


### [50] [Language Models as Measurement Apparatus for Culture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02459)
*Kent K. Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出NLP对文化的量化是一种物质-话语实践：语言模型并非被动记录文化现实，而是通过其设计选择（模型、数据、标注、评估）参与构成所测量的文化现象。借助Karen Barad的"能动切割"概念，论证测量装置与测量对象从一开始就相互纠缠，并以电视电影对话的三个案例研究和三个装置层面分析加以说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地被用于量化文化现象，但需要澄清什么使得这种测量具有"文化"独特性，即反思测量行为本身在文化构成中的参与角色。

Method: 理论论证结合案例研究：引入Karen Barad的"能动切割"概念作为分析框架，通过三个电视/电影对话案例（测量结构、互动和偏离）和三个装置层面考察（文化标记的擦除、对历史材料的调谐、能动工作流中的能动性）来说明核心论点。

Result: 展示了语言模型测量装置的设计选择如何划定文化现象与测量工具之间的边界，且这种边界从一开始就是纠缠的——模型已内化了大量它所测量的文化材料。

Conclusion: 提出一个理论驱动、经验严谨且文化依赖的研究纲领，将每一次"能动切割"视为兼具方法论与伦理性的自觉承诺。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly used to quantify cultural phenomena, but what makes such measurement distinctively cultural? This paper argues that NLP work on culture is a material-discursive practice: the apparatus -- model, data, annotation, evaluation -- participates in constituting the cultural reality it measures, rather than passively recording it. Drawing on Karen Barad's concept of the agential cut -- the contingent boundary between phenomenon and instrument -- I show that the apparatus's substantive design choices draw such boundaries, and that the boundary is entangled from the start because language models have already internalized much of the cultural material they measure. I illustrate this through three case studies on television and film dialogue (measuring structure, interaction, and deviation) and three examinations of the apparatus itself (erasure of cultural markers, attunement to historical material, and agency in an agentic workflow). This big picture analysis proposes a research program that is theory-driven, empirically rigorous, and culturally contingent, treating each agential cut as a conscious commitment, at once methodological and ethical.

</details>


### [51] [Will Scaling Improve Social Simulation with LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02464)
*Caleb Ziems,William Held,Su Doga Karaca,David Grusky,Tatsunori Hashimoto,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过缩放定律系统研究了LLM的计算规模与社会模拟保真度之间的关系。研究发现：在意见建模、行为模拟和纵向预测三个子领域中，计算规模与模拟保真度总体呈正相关（基于85个Qwen3架构模型），但存在重要例外——行为模拟中的认知偏差（风险厌恶）和学习启发式不随规模扩展而改善，纵向预测和低资源群体的意见建模扩展速度较慢。结论是规模扩展在多数场景下能提升社会模拟质量，但存在离群值且低资源领域改进不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM社会模拟虽有前景但保真度不足，当前研究缺乏对"计算规模扩展是否自然提升模拟保真度"的系统性理解。作者旨在探究：缩放范式能否弥合模拟与现实之间的差距，还是模拟保真度与通用能力正交、需要专门研究。

Method: 使用缩放定律方法，分两步实验：(1) 在固定计算预算（10^18至10^20 FLOPs）下预训练85个Qwen3架构transformer LLM（基于DCLM语料库）；(2) 评估35个更大规模（达70B参数）的开源模型。通过损失预测下游准确率，分析计算规模与三种社会模拟任务（意见建模、行为模拟、纵向预测）保真度的关系。

Result: 多数行为与意见模拟任务随规模快速提升（尤其英语语料中良好代表的群体）；纵向预测和低资源意见建模扩展较慢，与MMLU等通用基准相关性低；行为模拟中风险厌恶等认知偏差和学习相关奖励的启发式不随规模扩展改善（0.5B至8B参数微调无效）。

Conclusion: 计算规模扩展在多数社会模拟场景有效，但存在离群值（如认知偏差类任务）；低资源领域改进不可靠，模拟保真度不完全正交于通用能力但存在特殊维度需要专门研究。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) social simulations are a promising research method, but they are not yet faithful enough to be adopted widely. In this work, we investigate whether the current scaling paradigm in language modeling is likely to close these gaps, or whether simulation fidelity is orthogonal to general capabilities and therefore deserving of more research attention. We use scaling laws to study the relationship between LLMs' compute scale, general capability benchmarks, and the fidelity of social simulation in three representative sub-domains: opinion modeling, behavioral simulation, and longitudinal forecasting. Surprisingly, we discover strong compute scaling in all three settings, using a suite of 85 transformer LLMs with the Qwen3 architecture pre-trained on the DCLM web text corpus under fixed-compute budgets from $10^{18}$ to $10^{20}$ FLOPs. Then we evaluate 35 larger and more capable open-weight models up to 70B parameters, allowing us to predict downstream accuracy from loss. This reveals that the majority of behavioral and opinion simulation tasks will rapidly improve with scale, particularly when they involve populations that are well-represented in English web corpora. Longitudinal forecasting and underrepresented opinions scale more slowly, especially when they are less correlated with general knowledge and reasoning benchmarks like MMLU. In behavior simulation, scaling fails to improve model calibration with human cognitive biases like risk aversion, as well as human heuristics like learning correlated rewards from related tasks. On these tasks, even fine-tuned models fail to noticeably scale up performance from 0.5B to 8B parameters. Taken together, we conclude that scale will improve social simulations in most settings, but outliers exist, and improvements will be less reliable in low-resource domains.

</details>


### [52] [Audio-Based Understanding of Audiobook Narration Appeal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02473)
*Shahar Elisha,Mariano Beguerisse-Díaz,Emmanouil Benetos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地通过计算分析方法研究有声书的叙述质量（如语调、节奏、响度）与消费数据（观看率）之间的关系，发现声学特征本身与有声书吸引力显著相关，且这种关联在不同体裁和作品中表现不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叙述质量对有声书听众的体验和内容理解至关重要，但其影响如何随体裁、作品和受众变化尚未被系统研究，缺乏计算层面的量化分析。

Method: 从LibriVox提取语调、节奏、响度等声学特征（使用预训练音频模型），结合消费数据（观看率）及体裁/作品信息，分析声学特征与吸引力的关联，并用更细粒度的专有参与度指标进行验证。

Result: 即使在控制作品效应后，声学信息仍与有声书吸引力存在稳健关联；进一步通过专有参与度指标验证了该发现。

Conclusion: 声学特征对有声书吸引力有独立于作品的显著影响，为数据驱动的有声书个性化推荐和旁白选角提供了潜在方法。

Abstract: Narration is central to the audiobook listening experience, shaping how listeners engage with and understand the content. This work explores how narration qualities shape an audiobook's appeal, noting that their effects can vary by genre, title, and audience. We extract vocal and acoustic features (e.g., tone, pace, loudness) from LibriVox using pre-trained audio models and analyse their relationship with consumption data (specifically, view-rate) and their interplay with genre and title. Despite limited consumption data, we find that acoustic information alone has a robust association with appeal, even after accounting for title effects. We further validate these findings using more nuanced proprietary engagement metrics. To our knowledge, this is the first systematic computational study linking narration qualities, genre, title, and audiobook consumption, highlighting the potential of data-driven insights to improve audiobook personalisation and narrator casting.

</details>


### [53] [Visually Grounded Self-Reflection for Vision-Language Models via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02490)
*Liyan Tang,Fangcong Yin,Greg Durrett*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出VRRL框架，通过强化学习训练让视觉语言模型在链式思维推理中实现基于视觉的自我反思，有效提升了分布外场景下的视觉定位和空间导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在链式思维推理中虽能进行自我反思（回顾并修正先前错误），但难以正确关注视觉输入，导致反馈无法转化为基于视觉的修正，尤其是在分布外图像上表现不佳。

Method: 提出VRRL强化学习训练框架，包含两个关键组件：(1) 随机掩码轨迹前缀，让模型学会从中期错误中恢复而非仅关注早期错误；(2) 从经验回放缓冲区引入缓冲回滚，让模型暴露于多样化的失败状态并学习修正。

Result: 在表格、图表视觉定位任务和空间导航基准上，VRRL显著提升了分布外精度，优于标准RL和面向反思的微调基线。

Conclusion: 通过强化学习中的轨迹掩码和缓冲回滚机制，能够有效激发视觉语言模型基于视觉输入的自我反思能力，在分布偏移场景下保持鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large vision-language models can reason over multimodal inputs by generating textual chains of thought (CoT). A key capability exhibited in CoT reasoning is self-reflection: revisiting earlier decisions and correcting previous errors. However, existing LVLMs often fail to properly attend to visual inputs during reflection, limiting their ability to translate feedback into grounded corrections, especially for out-of-distribution images. To address this issue, we propose a novel reinforcement learning training framework VRRL, with two components explicitly designed to elicit visually grounded self-reflection. First, we randomly mask trajectory prefixes during training to emphasize recovery from incorrect intermediate predictions rather than making early mistakes. Second, we introduce buffered roll-ins from an experience replay buffer to expose the model to diverse failure states that it must learn to correct. We evaluate our approach on visual grounding tasks involving tables and charts, as well as spatial navigation benchmarks. While off-the-shelf and conventionally fine-tuned models degrade substantially under distribution shift, our method substantially improves average out-of-distribution accuracy over standard RL and reflection-oriented fine-tuning baselines by using self-reflection effectively.

</details>


### [54] [Reasoning LLM Improves Speaker Recognition in Long-form TV Dramas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02504)
*Yuxuan Li,Lingxi Xie,Xinyue Huo,Jihao Qiu,Jiacheng Shao,Pengfei Chen,Jiannan Ge,Kaiwen Duan,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对长视频剧集中的说话人识别任务，构建了包含53.2万条标注对话、覆盖900+角色的DramaSR-532K基准数据集，并提出了基于大推理模型的多模态工具调用方法DramaSR-LRM，在短语句等声学生物特征不可靠的场景下显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频剧集的理解面临严峻挑战，准确将每句对白归因到对应角色（说话人识别）是解析复杂剧情的关键，但现有方法和数据在此任务上存在不足。

Method: (1) 构建DramaSR-532K大规模基准数据集，包含53.2万条标注对话、900+角色，融合听觉、语言和视觉多模态线索；(2) 提出DramaSR-LRM方法，基于大推理模型，通过多模态工具使用自主聚合上下文证据，实现高保真度的说话人归因。

Result: DramaSR-LRM显著优于现有基线方法，尤其在短语句（声学生物特征不可靠）场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: 多模态信息融合与大推理模型的自适应证据聚合能有效提升长视频剧集中的说话人识别性能，数据和代码将开源。

Abstract: Long-form TV dramas present a formidable challenge for comprehensive video understanding, where deciphering complex storyline often relies on \textbf{speaker recognition}, the task of accurately attributing each spoken utterance to its respective character. In this paper, we advance this field through two primary contributions. (1) We introduce \textbf{DramaSR-532K}, a large-scale benchmark comprising 532K annotated dialogue lines across more than 900 unique characters, necessitating the integration of auditory, linguistic, and visual cues for speaker recognition. (2) We propose \textbf{DramaSR-LRM}, a robust approach built upon a large reasoning model (LRM). DramaSR-LRM is designed to autonomously aggregate contextual evidence via multimodal tool-use, synthesizing diverse inputs to achieve high-fidelity attribution. Experimental results demonstrate that DramaSR-LRM significantly outperforms existing baselines, particularly on short utterances where acoustic biometrics are inherently unreliable. \textit{All the data and code will be made publicly available at the project page: https://www.github.com/198808xc/DramaSR-LRM.}

</details>


### [55] [LACUNA: A Testbed for Evaluating Localization Precision for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02513)
*Matteo Boglioni,Thibault Rousset,Siva Reddy,Marius Mosbach,Verna Dankers*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LACUNA是一个新的机器遗忘测试平台，通过将合成PII注入预定义模型参数来提供参数级别的遗忘真值，实验表明现有SOTA遗忘方法虽在输出层面表现良好，但参数层面定位不精确且易受重新浮现攻击，而精确参数定位能显著提升遗忘效果和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗忘基准仅在输出层面评估，无法判断是否真正从参数中擦除知识还是仅仅掩盖了知识，而重新浮现攻击的成功进一步凸显了这一问题。

Method: LACUNA通过掩码连续预训练将合成个体的PII注入OLMo-based模型（1B和7B）的预定义参数中，提供参数级别的遗忘真值；然后利用该平台评估当前SOTA遗忘方法的参数定位精度和抗重新浮现攻击能力。

Result: 现有SOTA遗忘方法在输出层面表现良好，但参数层面高度不精确且易受重新浮现攻击；当定位成功时，即使简单的基于梯度的遗忘方法也能实现强擦除和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 精确的参数定位对于有效的机器遗忘至关重要，LACUNA为推进基于定位的鲁棒遗忘研究提供了重要的评估平台。

Abstract: LLMs memorize sensitive training data, including personally identifiable information (PII), creating a pressing need for reliable post hoc removal methods. Unlearning has emerged as a promising solution, with state-of-the-art(SOTA) methods often following a localize-first, unlearn-second paradigm that targets specific model parameters. However, existing benchmarks evaluate unlearning solely at the output level, leaving open the question of whether unlearning truly erases knowledge from a model's parameters or merely obfuscates it, a concern reinforced by the success of resurfacing attacks. To bridge this gap, we introduce LACUNA: the first unlearning testbed with ground-truth parameter-level localization. LACUNA injects PII of synthetic individuals into predefined parameters of 1B and 7B OLMo-based models via masked continual pretraining, enabling direct evaluation of whether unlearning targets the weights responsible for knowledge storage. We use LACUNA to benchmark current SOTA unlearning methods and find that, despite strong output-level performance, existing methods are highly imprecise and susceptible to resurfacing attacks. We further show that when localization is successful, even a simple gradient-based unlearning method achieves strong erasure and robustness to resurfacing attacks, highlighting the importance of precise unlearning. We release LACUNA to complement behavioral evaluations and drive further advances in robust, localization-based unlearning.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

### [56] [Multilayer Q-Matrix-Embedded Neural Network for Cognitive Diagnosis (M-QCDNet): Structure-Aware Deep Learning Architecture for Psychometric Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01278)
*Yiyao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种多层Q矩阵嵌入神经网络（M-QCDNet），将认知诊断模型的结构可解释性与深度学习神经网络相结合，通过Q矩阵结构先验、L2惩罚损失函数和可解释对齐指标，实现兼具预测性能与认知理论一致性的智能诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知诊断模型（CDMs）具有良好的结构可解释性但缺乏深层学习能力，而深度神经网络虽具备强大拟合能力却难以保证认知理论的一致性。作者旨在桥接两者，实现兼具心理测量透明性与神经网络灵活性的认知诊断方法。

Method: 1) 将Q矩阵作为结构先验嵌入多层神经网络，确保潜在掌握特征的可解释性；2) 设计带L2惩罚的损失函数，对与Q矩阵不一致的技能进行惩罚，平衡预测性能与结构对齐；3) 开发基于可解释对齐的评估指标，量化预测技能激活与项目级技能的一致性。

Result: M-QCDNet在保持预测性能的同时，显著提升了技能激活模式与Q矩阵定义的理论结构之间的一致性，实现了可解释的对齐度量。

Conclusion: M-QCDNet成功融合了心理测量学的透明性与神经网络的灵活性，为课堂实践提供了早期学习困难检测和掌握式干预支持，推动了可解释、公平且可操作的认知诊断AI发展。

Abstract: The research proposes a multilayer Q-matrix-embedded neural network for cognitive diagnosis (M-QCDNet), which integrates the structural interpretability of cognitive diagnostic models (CDMs) with the deep learning neural network (NN). M-QCDNet structures the item-skill relationship using the Q-matrix as a structural prior, ensuring latent mastery profiles remain interpretable and consistent with cognitive theory, followed by the proposed loss function with an L2 penalty to penalize skills not aligned with the Q-matrix and to balance predictive performance and structural alignment. Corresponding evaluation matrices, the interpretable alignment-based metrics that quantify the degree to which predicted skill activations correspond to item-level skills, were further developed. M-QCDNet offers practical benefits for classroom practice, enabling early detection of learning difficulties and supporting mastery-based interventions. By embedding diagnostic validity into model design, M-QCDNet bridges psychometric transparency and neural flexibility, advancing interpretable, fair, and actionable AI for cognitive diagnostics.

</details>


### [57] [I\textsuperscript{2}RiMA: Spectral Riemannian Representation with Temporal Attention for Mental Stress Detection based on EEG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01279)
*Cheng He,Kunyu Peng,Shangen Han,Jinming Ma,Jinhong Ding,Likun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于流形内-间注意力网络的脑电情绪识别方法（IIRMANet），通过在频域构建SPD协方差矩阵、聚类频率成分并融合局部与全局时序信息，在三个数据集上以1.60M参数和31.95M FLOPs实现了最高82.78%的平衡准确率，优于五种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨被试脑电压力检测中，判别性压力模式既依赖被试个体又具有频率特异性。现有黎曼方法主要在时域建模空间协方差，忽略了关键的神经振荡信息；而标准时间分词化常破坏切片间的时间连贯性。

Method: 提出IIRMANet：1) 在每个频率点独立构建空间协方差矩阵并映射到SPD切空间，保留通道间几何结构与频率特异性判别信息；2) 频率聚类聚合，选择信息性频谱成分并按脑电节律形成紧凑数据驱动的频率簇；3) 内-间切片注意力模块，自适应集成局部切片级频谱动力学与全局时序上下文。

Result: 在三个数据集上，IIRMANet持续优于五种最先进基线，最高达82.78%平衡准确率，参数量仅1.60M，FLOPs为31.95M。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过频域黎曼流形建模与多尺度注意力机制，有效解决了跨被试脑电压力检测中频率特异性与被试差异问题，兼具高精度与计算效率。

Abstract: Cross-subject EEG stress detection remains challenging because discriminative stress-related patterns are both subject-dependent and frequency-specific. Conventional Riemannian methods model spatial covariance mainly in the time domain, overlooking neural oscillations that are critical for high-level cognitive state decoding, while standard temporal tokenization often fragments inter-slice temporal coherence. To address these limitations, we propose \method{}, an Intra-Inter Riemannian Manifold Attention Network for EEG-based stress detection. \method{} constructs spatial covariance matrices independently at each frequency point and maps them to the SPD tangent space, preserving channel-wise geometry together with frequency-specific discriminative cues. It further introduces frequency cluster aggregation to select informative spectral components and reduce redundancy by forming compact, data-driven frequency clusters aligned with EEG rhythms. Finally, an intra-inter slice attention module adaptively integrates local slice-level spectral dynamics and global temporal context across EEG sequences. Experiments on three datasets show that \method{} consistently outperforms five state-of-the-art baselines, achieving up to 82.78\% balanced accuracy while remaining efficient with only 1.60M parameters and 31.95M FLOPs.

</details>


### [58] [Fixed-Set Robustness in Programming by Example: Example Corruption and Semantic Partition Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01280)
*Yuan Si,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究编程-by-示例(PBE)系统的对抗鲁棒性，提出了一种新的攻击范式：攻击者针对合成器选择性地破坏示例，使返回程序质量受损。作者形式化定义了固定集最坏情况破坏问题，实现了针对字符串转换DSL的精确和启发式攻击搜索，并提出了基于版本空间分区聚合(VPA)的防御机制。实验表明，低裕度PBE任务存在随机噪声评估无法覆盖的对抗鲁棒性维度，而VPA仅在语义投票裕度足够时有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PBE系统大多假设错误示例来自随机噪声过程并通过最小化期望损失来处理，忽略了对抗性攻击场景——即攻击者观察合成器后恶意选择破坏哪些示例，导致返回程序质量严重下降。本文旨在揭示这一被忽视的对抗鲁棒性维度。

Method: 1) 形式化定义固定集最坏情况破坏问题；2) 在字符串转换DSL上实现有界池精确搜索和启发式破坏搜索；3) 提出版本空间分区聚合(VPA)防御机制，将示例分为不相交组分别合成，再通过语义签名投票聚合结果；4) 在多种基准上评估，包括人工/生成DSL任务、公开SyGuS PBE_SLIA、SYNTRA Playgol v2以及噪声PBE基线。

Result: 1) 一个精心设计的编辑就能翻转所有8个spike任务，而200次随机控制仅能翻转10.3%-16.7%；2) 生成的低裕度任务在预算1下即可被翻转，但VPA能恢复；3) 公开SyGuS任务投票裕度接近1，自适应攻击可使VPA准确率降至零；4) Playgol在141个接受行上对typo和随机控制有正向配对引导差距；5) 20个裕度-1任务上，本地和API模型均呈现相同的高质量-被攻击模式。

Conclusion: PBE系统存在对抗鲁棒性维度，随机噪声评估不能覆盖这一维度。VPA防御仅在干净语义保持分区投票裕度时有效，而这在现实任务中往往难以满足。该发现是有界的、部分负面的——揭示了PBE系统在面对对抗性示例选择时的脆弱性。

Abstract: Programming-by-example systems infer programs from a small set of input-output examples. Robust PBE work usually models wrong examples as samples from a stochastic noise process and then minimizes an expected or empirical loss. This paper studies a different failure mode: an adversary who sees the synthesizer and chooses the examples whose corruption most damages the returned program. We formalize fixed-set worst-case corruption for finite PBE version spaces, implement exact-within-bounded-pool and heuristic corruption searches for a string-transformation DSL, and introduce version-space partition aggregation (VPA), a defense that synthesizes on disjoint example groups and votes by semantic signatures. The central claim is deliberately bounded and partly negative: low-margin PBE tasks have an adversarial robustness dimension that random-typo and noisy-PBE evaluations miss, while semantic partition aggregation helps only when the clean semantics keep a partition vote margin, which often fails on realistic tasks. Evidence from curated/generated DSL tasks, accepted public SyGuS PBE_SLIA slices, SYNTRA Playgol v2, and noisy-PBE objective baselines supports that boundary. One curated edit flips all 8 spike tasks while 200-trial typo, DSL-pool, and distance-matched random controls succeed on 10.3%, 11.0%, and 16.7%; generated margin-1 rows flip under budget 1 yet VPA recovers them; on public SyGuS the vote margin is near one, so an adaptive attacker drives VPA accuracy to zero; accepted public SyGuS slices move across exact-within-pool budget boundaries; and Playgol shows positive paired-bootstrap gaps against typo and same-pool random controls on the 141 accepted rows. A small exact-output prompt harness over 20 controlled margin-1 tasks shows the same qualitative clean-to-attacked pattern across local and API models, while it is treated as a scope check, not a broad LLM benchmark.

</details>


### [59] [Domain Knowledge Based Temporal-Spatial Graph Convolution Network for ECG Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01282)
*Wenting Ma,Zhipeng Zhang,Xiaohang Yuan,Ningwei Xie,Yuxin Xie,Xiaolin Wang,Meng Guo,Xingang Chai,Zhenjie Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于领域知识（PRQST关键点）的双流有向图卷积网络用于ECG识别，通过空间有向图建模关键点位置关系、时间有向图建模周期间依赖，在首届中国心电图智能竞赛数据集上九分类任务中取得88.1%平均F1和76.3%稀有类别F1，优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型在医疗等专业领域（如ECG识别）中可解释性不足，端到端卷积神经网络难以融入领域知识，导致对稀有类别检测性能不佳。

Method: 提出基于领域知识的双流有向图卷积网络：1) 将ECG关键点PRQST作为领域知识；2) 空间有向图建模关键点间位置关系；3) 时间有向图建模相邻周期间时序依赖。

Result: 在首届中国心电图智能竞赛数据集上，九分类任务整体平均F1为88.1%，稀有类别平均F1为76.3%，均优于现有最优模型。

Conclusion: 领域知识的引入有效提升了ECG识别性能，尤其对稀有类别检测效果显著，验证了图卷积网络结合领域知识在医疗AI中的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: In light of strides in Arti cial Intelligence (AI) and its wide spread application, challenges persist in the interpretability of AI models, particularly within specialized domains like healthcare, such as electro cardiograph (ECG) recognition. Rather than relying solely on end-to-end convolutional neural networks, this paper introduces a novel approach using a domain knowledge-based graph convolution network for ECG recognition. Key landmarks points of PRQST, vital to ECG interpreta tion, are incorporated as domain knowledge. The double-stream directed graph is employed to model both intra and inter ECG cycles. Speci cally, spatial directed graphs capture the positional relationships among key points, while temporal directed graphs delineate temporal dependencies between adjacent cycles in extended ECG sequences. Experimental re sults on the First Chinese ECG Intelligent Competition dataset, which speci cally classify ECG into nine categories, prove the e cacy of the proposed model. The overall average F1 score is 88.1%, the average F1 score of rare categories is 76.3%, both outperform the state-of-the-art models. The introduction of domain knowledge did enhance the detec tion performance, especially for rare categories.

</details>


### [60] [Scaling Laws for Grid-Based Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01283)
*Matthew J Liu,Wei Hang Zheng,Vidhan Purohit,Siqi Xie,Chieh-En Li,Jerry Li,Noah Flynn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多探针网格算法在ANN搜索中的性能，发现在GloVe嵌入族中存在维度缩放交叉现象：多探针网格保持近似恒定的维度缩放指数，而图、树和分区方法吞吐量下降。该方法在N上接近线性查询缩放，且索引成本低于竞争方法，适用于重建频繁或高维场景。此外，相关工作已将自注意力形式化为ANN操作，因此ANN算法的缩放特性可用于分析高效Transformer架构的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于网格的ANN方法在现代表现分析中被忽视，缺乏系统性的缩放特性研究，尤其是在高维和大规模数据集下的性能表征。

Method: 对多探针网格算法在数据集大小N和维度d两个维度上进行系统实验表征，并与图、树和分区方法进行对比。

Result: 发现GloVe嵌入族中存在d-缩放交叉现象：多探针网格维持近似恒定的维度缩放指数，而其他方法吞吐量下降；多探针网格在N上接近线性查询缩放，且索引成本更低。

Conclusion: 多探针网格方法在重建频繁或高维设置中具有竞争力；其缩放特性可用于分析高效Transformer架构的成本。

Abstract: Grid-based approaches to approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search have been absent from modern scaling analyses. We present a systematic characterization of a multiprobe grid algorithm with respect to dataset size $N$ and dimensionality $d$. Our experiments reveal a previously unreported $d$-scaling crossover on the GloVe embedding family, in which multiprobe grid search maintains an approximately constant dimensional scaling exponent while other graph-, tree-, and partitioning-based methods exhibit degrading throughput. The advantage comes with near-linear query scaling in $N$, but also with lower indexing cost than competing ANN methods. Our results suggest that grid-based methods such as multiprobe grid may be competitive in rebuild-heavy or high-dimensional settings where indexing cost and dimensional robustness dictate performance. More broadly, recent work has formalized self-attention as an ANN operation. Thus, the $N$- and $d$-scaling properties of ANN algorithms may guide cost analysis of efficient transformer architectures. Code is available at: https://github.com/weiz345/MultiProbeANN.

</details>

Method: 基于EV-Battery-IonSense索引，为公开电池数据集记录添加量子相关元数据，包括任务类型、感知模态、化学体系、标签可用性、序列类型、预处理需求、候选量子编码、估计量子比特范围和NISQ可行性。引入量子就绪评分（Quantum Readiness Score）作为数据集选择的启发式方法，并提供元数据表、评分脚本、鲁棒性检查、链接检查工具和SQL风格查询示例。

Result: 实现了基于查询的数据集发现能力，能够识别适合紧凑量子特征映射、量子时间序列工作流、有限标签异常检测和未来电池健康基准测试的数据集。发布了包含元数据表、评分脚本、鲁棒性检查、链接检查工具和SQL风格查询示例的完整工具包。

Conclusion: IonSense-QKG将数据集选择定位为数据管理问题，为数据驱动的量子电池分析提供了可复现的基础，有助于推动混合量子-经典方法在电池健康管理领域的应用。

Abstract: Public lithium-ion battery datasets are increasingly used for state-of-health estimation, remaining-useful-life prediction, anomaly detection, electrochemical diagnostics, second-life analytics, and battery safety research. However, these datasets vary substantially in chemistry, modality, scale, label quality, sequence structure, access status, and preprocessing complexity. These differences directly affect whether a dataset is feasible for near-term hybrid quantum-classical machine-learning workflows.
  This paper presents IonSense-QKG, a quantum-readiness metadata framework for lithium-ion battery dataset discovery. Starting from the EV-Battery-IonSense index, the proposed framework enriches public battery dataset records with quantum-relevant metadata, including task type, sensing modality, chemistry, label availability, sequence type, preprocessing requirements, candidate quantum encodings, estimated qubit range, and NISQ feasibility. A transparent Quantum Readiness Score is introduced to rank datasets as candidate resources for future hybrid quantum-classical battery benchmarks. The score is intended as a dataset-selection heuristic, not as evidence of quantum advantage.
  The framework demonstrates query-based discovery over enriched metadata to identify datasets suitable for compact quantum feature maps, quantum time-series workflows, limited-label anomaly detection, and future battery-health benchmarking. The released artifact includes metadata tables, scoring scripts, robustness checks, link-checking utilities, and SQL-style query examples. IonSense-QKG positions dataset selection as a data-management problem and provides a reproducible foundation for data-centric quantum battery analytics.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNA甲基化分析已成为中枢神经系统肿瘤分类的强大工具，但跨队列可迁移性、方法学正确性及稳健的多类评估仍存在重要挑战，亟需更可靠的方法学方案。

Method: 采用稀疏随机投影(Sparse Random Projection)进行降维，结合多项逻辑回归(Multinomial Logistic Regression)进行分类，在与广泛使用的参考分类器相同的实验设置下进行评估。

Result: 在2,801样本参考队列上，分层3折交叉验证平均准确率96%；在1,104独立临床队列上，91类级别准确率86%，甲基化家族级别准确率93%，相比现有最优的82%和88%分别提升约4和5个百分点。

Conclusion: 该方法在更强的机器学习方法学实践基础上，一致性地超越了现有最优性能，能够实质性提升CNS肿瘤分类的可靠性，对癌症亚型分配及后续治疗选择具有临床意义。

Abstract: NA methylation profiling has become a powerful approach for central nervous system (CNS) tumor classification, yet important challenges remain regarding cross-cohort transferability, methodological correctness, and robust multiclass evaluation. In this work, we propose a novel and methodologically rigorous machine-learning approach for methylation-based CNS tumor classification that combines Sparse Random Projection for dimensionality reduction with multinomial logistic regression for classification. We evaluate the proposed approach in the same general experimental setting established by a widely used reference classifier. On the 2,801-sample reference cohort, our method achieves a mean accuracy of 96\% under stratified 3-fold cross-validation. On the independent 1,104-sample clinical evaluation cohort, it reaches 86\% accuracy at the 91-class level and 93\% when predictions are evaluated at the methylation class family level. These results improve upon the corresponding state-of-the-art reference figures of 82\% class-level concordance and 88\% family-level concordance, yielding absolute gains of approximately 4 and 5 percentage points, respectively. This improvement is clinically relevant: in a diagnostic setting, a 5-point increase in correct tumor classification can directly affect cancer subtype assignment and, in turn, influence treatment selection and downstream clinical decision-making. Our results show that the proposed model, grounded in stronger methodological practice in machine learning, consistently outperforms the previous state of the art across evaluation settings and can materially improve the reliability of CNS tumor classification.

</details>


### [63] [From Approximation to Emergence: A Theory of Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01311)
*Zhilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从经典逼近论、优化和泛化出发，系统梳理了过参数化、鲁棒性、生成建模、Transformer、上下文学习、缩放定律、可解释性、对齐和涌现等现代深度学习理论，为研究者提供了一份严谨的理论地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习已超出任何单一数学解释的范畴，需要一个统一、基于证明的理论框架来整合分散的研究成果，并厘清各理论之间的逻辑关系及其未解释的现象。

Method: 采用统一的研究叙事，以每个理论所控制的对象、使其成立的假设以及其无法解释的现象为分析框架，系统梳理从经典到现代的深度学习理论文献。

Result: 呈现了深度学习理论的完整性现状：理论强大但尚不完整，核心问题聚焦于学习机制如何从规模、数据、架构和训练中涌现。

Conclusion: 深度学习理论正处于从分散结果向统一科学叙事转变的过程中，涌现问题成为当前理论研究的中心议题。

Abstract: Deep learning has outgrown any single mathematical explanation. From Approximation to Emergence develops a unified, proof-oriented account of modern deep learning theory, tracing a path from the classical foundations of approximation, optimization, and generalization to the contemporary mechanisms of overparameterization, robustness, generative modeling, transformers, in-context learning, scaling laws, interpretability, alignment, and emergence. Rather than presenting isolated results, the book organizes a broad literature into a coherent research narrative: each theory is examined through the object it controls, the assumptions that make it valid, and the phenomena it leaves unexplained. Written for researchers, graduate students, and mathematically trained practitioners, this monograph offers a rigorous map of deep learning theory as it stands today: powerful, incomplete, and increasingly centered on the question of how learned mechanisms arise from scale, data, architecture, and training.

</details>


### [64] [Black-Box Inference of LLM Architectural Properties with Restrictive API Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01313)
*Christopher Ellis,Shreyas Chaudhari,Mei-Yu Wang,Leighton Barnes,Giulia Fanti,José M. F. Moura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为NightVision的攻击方法，即使在当前限制严格的LLM API（仅返回单个logit且无bias功能）条件下，仍能通过共集提示技术和首令牌时间（TTFT）测量，有效估计模型的隐藏维度、深度和参数量。实验表明，该方法对32个开源LLM的隐藏维度平均相对误差在23%以内（MoE模型为9%），参数超过30亿的模型深度和参数量估计误差在53%以内，揭示了当前API限制不足以完全隐藏模型架构细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业LLM供应商为保护模型架构隐私，限制了API仅返回单个解码token的logit并移除logit bias功能。然而，现有研究表明较宽松的API可恢复部分架构参数。本文旨在验证在更严格API限制下，攻击者是否仍能推断出模型的架构参数。

Method: NightVision方法包含两个核心步骤：(1) 共集提示（common set prompting）技术：构造多个提示，使输出暴露同一组token的log概率，再通过谱分析推断隐藏维度；(2) 利用端到端首令牌时间（TTFT）测量结合估计的隐藏维度，进一步估计模型深度和总参数量。

Result: 在32个开源LLM上，隐藏维度估计平均相对误差23%（MoE模型9%）；超30亿参数模型的深度和参数量估计误差在53%以内。消融实验展示了估计精度随token预算和模型属性的变化规律。

Conclusion: 当前LLM API的限制不足以完全隐藏底层模型的架构细节，商业供应商可能需要采取更严格的防护措施。

Abstract: In practice, most commercial LLM providers do not publicly release details of underlying LLM architectures. However, prior work has shown that given limited API access to an LLM (namely, top-$k$ logits and/or a logit bias function), one can recover certain architectural details of an LLM, such as the hidden dimension of the feed-forward network. Perhaps in response to these results, most commercial LLM providers have restricted their APIs to expose only the single logit for each decoded token, and they no longer give users the ability to bias logits. We show that even under current restrictive APIs, several architectural parameters are still recoverable. We present NightVision, an attack that uses restrictive black-box API access to estimate the hidden dimension, depth, and parameter count of an LLM. Algorithmically, NightVision relies on a novel common set prompting technique in which multiple prompts expose log probabilities for the same set of output tokens; a spectral analysis of these results is used to infer hidden dimension. NightVision additionally uses end-to-end time to first token (TTFT) measurements and the estimated hidden dimension to estimate depth and parameter count. We empirically evaluate NightVision on 32 open-source LLMs, recovering hidden dimension to within 23% average relative error across all models (9% on MoE models), and depth and parameter count to within 53% for models exceeding three billion parameters. We run extensive ablations to demonstrate how these accuracies scale with token budget and model properties. Overall, our results suggest that current LLM APIs are not sufficiently restricted to fully obfuscate the architectural details of their underlying models.

</details>


### [65] [Regularized Variational and Spectral Log-Density-Ratio Estimation in the Gaussian Location Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01895)
*Francis Bach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在高斯位置模型中研究了岭正则化对数密度比估计，推导了变分估计器和谱估计器在高维确定性极限下的渐近等价形式，并比较了二者的总体风险：在观测充足时变分估计器风险更小，在观测不足时谱估计器因协方差构造方差更低而更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高斯位置模型（共同协方差矩阵）下，研究岭正则化的对数密度比估计方法，推导变分估计器和谱估计器在高维渐近极限下的风险特性，并比较二者的适用场景，同时探索核范数惩罚用于特征学习的可能性。

Method: 利用仿射不变性将模型标准化为 q~N(0,I), p~N(Δ,I) 的线性特征形式。变分估计器采用经验KL对数归一化拟合加L2惩罚；谱估计器来自[1]中通过连续岭正则化最小二乘问题替代单一变分问题。推导采用凸-高斯-极小-极大定理（CGMT）和解析延拓方法得到确定性渐近等价形式。

Result: 得到了变分估计器和谱估计器在高维渐近极限下的确定性等价表达式；在观测充足时变分估计器风险更小，在观测不足时谱估计器因方差更低而更优；同时部分分析了核范数惩罚在特征学习中的应用。

Conclusion: 在共同协方差矩阵的高斯位置模型下，岭正则化对数密度比估计中，变分估计器适用于大样本场景，谱估计器适用于小样本场景；核范数惩罚可用于特征学习但需进一步分析。

Abstract: We study ridge-regularized log-density-ratio estimation in the Gaussian location model with a common covariance matrix. By affine invariance, the model is written as q $\sim$ N(0, I), p $\sim$ N($Δ$, I), with linear features, where $Δ$ is a mean vector. The variational estimator is the empirical Kullback-Leibler (KL) log-normalized fit with a squared L2-penalty on its nonconstant coefficient, and the spectral estimator recently introduced in [1] replaces a single variational problem by a continuum of ridge-regularized least-squares problems. We derive high-dimensional deterministic asymptotic equivalents when the numbers of observations and dimension tend to infinity with fixed ratios. The regularized variational limit is characterized by a scalar entropy minimization problem derived from the convex-Gaussian-min-max theorem (CGMT), while the regularized spectral limit follows from deterministic equivalents for resolvents of weighted sums of two independent Gaussian sample covariance matrices. We use these formulas to compare population risks, with experiments focused on fixed-signal aspect-ratio sweeps and optimized regularization. Our conclusion is that with many observations, under the criteria and asymptotic regimes analyzed here, the well-specified variational estimator has the smaller risk, while with fewer observations, the spectral estimator is favored because its covariance-based construction has lower variance. We also study how a nuclear penalty can be used and partially analyzed to perform feature learning.

</details>


### [66] [Multi-modal Rail Crossing Safety Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01365)
*Paimon Goulart,Chansong Lim,Nícolas Roque dos Santos,Yue Dong,Sheldon Peterson,Jia Chen,Evangelos E. Papalexakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态AI管道，通过结合铁路道口的视觉图像和历史事故数据，评估其安全风险。该系统使用路由微调紧凑型VLM管道，能以0.757的宏F1分数识别高/低风险道口，并以RMSE 0.078和相关性0.492估计FRA安全评分，结果与专家评估一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否利用视觉线索稳健估计铁路道口的安全性，以及引入结构化事故数据是否能提升评估能力，最终构建一个与专家意见和FRA安全评分一致的多模态AI安全评估系统。

Method: 提出一个概念验证管道，涵盖数据准备到多种学习范式，采用路由微调紧凑型视觉语言模型（VLM）处理多模态数据（道口图像+事故报告），输出安全评估和评分。

Result: 系统识别高风险和低风险道口的宏F1分数为0.757；估计FRA安全评分的RMSE为0.078，相关系数为0.492；定性结果与领域专家评估一致。

Conclusion: 通过视觉线索和结构化事故数据的多模态融合，能够有效评估铁路道口的安全风险，且微调紧凑型VLM管道在性能与专家对齐方面表现良好，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Given one or more images of a railway crossing, can we leverage visual cues that allow us to robustly estimate how safe it is? Can we improve our ability to do so by introducing structured data (such as official accident reports) about the accident history of that crossing into our models? In this work, we explore how to best answer those questions towards building an AI system that can ingest multi-modal data for railway crossings and provide safety assessment and scores that align with expert opinion and with safety scoring used by the Federal Railroad Administration (FRA). To that end, we propose a proof-of-concept pipeline that delivers on that goal, while at the same time exploring and tackling a number of critical research challenges that pertain to different parts of the pipeline, from data preparation to different learning paradigms that can allow us to realize such a system. Indicatively, our proposed system identifies HIGH-RISK and LOW-RISK crossings with a macro F1 score of 0.757 and estimates FRA-based safety scores with an RMSE of 0.078 and correlation of 0.492 using a routed fine-tuned compact VLM pipeline, while producing qualitative results that align with domain-expert assessment.

</details>


### [67] [ART for Diffusion Sampling: Continuous-Time Control and Actor-Critic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02137)
*Yilie Huang,Wenpin Tang,Xun Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ART-RL，一种通过强化学习自适应分配扩散模型采样时间步的方法，能够在保持采样管道不变的情况下，仅通过改变时间步网格持续提升样本质量，并展现出良好的跨预算、数据集和求解器的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型采样时间步分配依赖于均匀网格或手工设计的固定调度，这些固定方案可能是次优的，缺乏自适应调整能力。

Method: 提出ART（自适应重参数化时间），将时间变化率作为控制变量的连续时间控制框架，使均匀网格在学习到的时钟上对应原始扩散时间的自适应步长。引入ART-RL，通过高斯策略将调度学习转化为连续时间强化学习问题，并证明其与ART在优化器层面等价，推导出可实现的演员-评论家更新规则。

Result: 在从低维控制到图像生成的多个实验中，ART-RL仅通过改变时间步网格即可持续提升采样质量，优于强基线调度；学习到的调度具有广泛的泛化能力，可在不同采样预算、数据集、求解器、管道和表示空间之间迁移。

Conclusion: 自适应时间步分配能显著提升扩散模型采样质量，且学习到的调度具有良好的泛化性和即插即用特性。

Abstract: We study timestep allocation for score-based diffusion sampling, where a learned reverse-time dynamics is discretized on a finite grid. Uniform and hand-crafted schedules are standard choices, but they rely on fixed prescriptions and can therefore be suboptimal. To address this limitation, we propose Adaptive Reparameterized Time (ART), a continuous-time control formulation that learns a time change by treating the speed of the sampling clock as the control, so that a uniform grid on the learned clock induces adaptive timesteps in the original diffusion time. Based on a leading-order Euler error surrogate, ART provides a principled objective for allocating timesteps along the sampling trajectory. To solve this deterministic control problem, we introduce ART-RL, an auxiliary randomized formulation with Gaussian policies that turns schedule learning into a continuous-time reinforcement learning problem. We prove that the randomized ART-RL formulation is equivalent to ART at the optimizer level, in the sense that its optimal Gaussian policy recovers the optimal ART time-warping rate through its mean. We further establish policy evaluation and policy improvement characterizations and derive trajectory-based moment identities that yield implementable actor--critic updates for learning the schedule. Across experiments ranging from controlled low-dimensional settings to image generation, ART-RL can be plugged into existing diffusion samplers by changing only the timestep grid, consistently improving sample quality over strong baseline schedules at matched budgets while leaving the rest of the sampling pipeline unchanged. The learned schedules also exhibit broad generalization, transferring without retraining across sampling budgets, datasets, solvers, pipelines, and representation spaces.

</details>


### [68] [How Should Transformers Encode Numeric Values in Electronic Health Records?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01391)
*Maria Elkjær Montgomery,Christian Igel,Mikkel Odgaard,Martin Sillesen,Mads Nielsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了Transformer在电子健康记录数据中编码数值的三种策略（离散、连续、混合），发现混合令牌方法在精度敏感任务中表现最佳，实际应用中鲁棒性和可部署性通常优于最大数值精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在如何处理EHR数据中的数值型特征（如实验室检验值）缺乏系统性比较，不同编码策略在精度、优化稳定性和架构灵活性间存在权衡，需要明确指导实践选择。

Method: 在合成算术任务（嵌入真实EHR数据）和真实临床预测任务上，系统比较了离散编码、连续编码和混合编码三种数值编码策略的性能。

Result: 1）显式建模值-概念交互的方法在精度敏感算术任务上表现最佳但有架构限制；2）混合令牌方法（保留数值但先分箱再投影）鲁棒性更好，最优分箱数遵循关于数据集大小的幂律关系；3）模型表现"足够好"的数值计算而非精确算术；4）临床任务中引入实验室值的收益具有任务依赖性。

Conclusion: 鲁棒性和可部署性在实际应用中通常比最大数值精度更重要，混合令牌方法被推荐为实际默认方案。

Abstract: How do we encode numeric values in transformer-based sequence processing, particularly in electronic health record (EHR) data? We systematically compare discrete, continuous, and hybrid value encoding strategies using synthetic arithmetic tasks embedded within real-world EHR data, as well as real-world clinical prediction tasks. Our study reveals trade-offs between numeric precision, optimisation stability, and architectural flexibility. We find that approaches that explicitly model value-concept interactions perform best on precision-sensitive arithmetic tasks when architectural constraints permit. Hybrid token-based approaches that retain numeric values but apply binning prior to projection provide a more robust and broadly applicable alternative, with the optimal number of bins following a simple empirically derived power-law in dataset size. Across tasks, models consistently exhibit reliable "good enough" numeric computation rather than exact arithmetic, while clinical gains from incorporating laboratory values are task-dependent. This suggests that robustness and deployability often outweigh maximal numeric precision in practice, motivating hybrid token-based approaches as a practical default.

</details>


### [69] [An Optimisation Framework for the Well-Conditioned Training of Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02194)
*Joseph Webb,Sadok Jerad,Coralia Cartis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DSGNAR是一个双草图高斯-牛顿自适应比率优化框架，通过解决物理信息神经网络的严重病态损失景观问题，在精度和速度上显著超越现有方法，可达双精度3×10^{-16}的相对ℓ2误差，在Burgers方程上提升五个数量级，在高维泊松问题上提升八个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息神经网络在求解偏微分方程时精度远不及经典数值求解器，其根本障碍在于优化过程中的严重病态损失景观问题。

Method: 提出DSGNAR框架，采用双草图高斯-牛顿模型结合自适应正则化与步长控制策略，构成可扩展的二阶优化方法。

Result: 在非线性、混沌、多尺度、高维及Navier-Stokes等基准问题上达到双精度ℓ2误差低至3×10^{-16}；Burgers方程提升五个数量级，高维泊松问题提升八个数量级；单精度下Burgers方程10秒内达到4.75×10^{-7}的ℓ2误差。

Conclusion: DSGNAR框架在架构选择、算术精度和初始超参数方面均表现出鲁棒性，显著缩小了PINNs与传统数值求解器之间的精度差距。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a promising route to solve partial differential equations, yet they have struggled to reach the precision of classical solvers. The obstacle is increasingly understood to be one of optimisation, owing to the severely ill-conditioned loss landscape. We present $\textbf{DSGNAR}$: Doubly-Sketched Gauss-Newton with Adaptive Ratio, a scalable second-order optimisation framework that confronts this ill-conditioning and, in doing so, obtains unprecedented accuracy and speed. $\textbf{DSGNAR}$ couples a doubly-sketched Gauss-Newton model with a novel strategy that carefully controls both regularisation and step length. Across a suite of problems spanning nonlinear, chaotic, multi-scale, high-dimensional, and Navier-Stokes, the framework greatly improves on the state of the art: able to attain relative $\ell_2$ errors as low as $3\times10^{-16}$ in double precision, improve contemporary results by five orders of magnitude on the canonical Burgers' equation, and as much as eight orders on a high-dimensional Poisson problem, while remaining markedly faster. We further show that, in single precision, solutions at the limit of round-off error can be obtained very quickly: Burgers' equation to $\ell_2^{\text{rel}} = 4.75 \times 10^{-7}$ in under ten seconds. The framework is also robust to the choice of architecture, arithmetic precision, and initial hyperparameters.
  The code is available at https://www.github.com/wephy/physics-informed-neural-networks

</details>


### [70] [NeuroBridge: Bridging Multi-Task MRI Knowledge for Neurodegenerative Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01401)
*Mengyu Li,Guoyao Shen,Chad W. Farris,Xin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuroBridge是一个临床引导的多任务MRI框架，通过自监督预训练、海马分割/萎缩分类/重建等多任务学习及门控融合微调，在AD、MCI等神经退行性疾病诊断中达到SOTA性能（ADNI 88.17%，OASIS 82.78%），并展现了跨队列泛化能力和概率驱动的机会性筛查可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于MRI的阿尔茨海默病及相关痴呆诊断方法面临挑战，因为疾病相关的结构变化通常微小且异质性高，现有单任务方法难以充分捕捉这些复杂特征。

Method: NeuroBridge框架整合了三个关键组件：(1)大规模自监督MRI预训练；(2)多任务学习，包括海马分割、海马萎缩分类和重建目标；(3)门控融合（gated fusion）微调策略。

Result: 在ADNI和OASIS数据集上，AD vs 认知正常对照的分类准确率分别达到88.17%和82.78%；在MCI相关和混合诊断场景中改进最大；展现出强大的跨队列泛化能力，以及预测概率与准确性之间的系统性关联。

Conclusion: 临床引导的多任务表示学习优于传统的单任务方法，NeuroBridge为痴呆评估和MRI机会性筛查提供了鲁棒且可扩展的框架。

Abstract: INTRODUCTION: Accurate MRI-based identification of Alzheimer's disease (AD), mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and related dementias remains challenging because disease-related structural changes are often subtle and heterogeneous. We developed NeuroBridge, a clinically guided multi-task MRI framework for neurodegenerative disease diagnosis. METHODS: NeuroBridge integrates large-scale self-supervised MRI pretraining with hippocampal segmentation, hippocampal atrophy classification, and reconstruction objectives, followed by gated fusion fine-tuning. Performance was evaluated across ADNI and OASIS cohorts, including cross-cohort transfer, probability-based analysis, and opportunistic screening. RESULTS: NeuroBridge achieved the highest performance across evaluated classification tasks, reaching 88.17% accuracy for AD versus cognitively normal controls in ADNI and 82.78% in OASIS. The largest gains occurred in MCI-related and mixed-diagnosis settings. The framework demonstrated strong cross-cohort generalization, systematic associations between predicted-class probability and accuracy, and the feasibility of probability-based opportunistic screening. DISCUSSION: Clinically guided multi-task representation learning improves neurodegenerative MRI diagnosis beyond conventional single-task approaches. NeuroBridge provides a robust and scalable framework for dementia assessment and MRI-based opportunistic screening.

</details>


### [71] [Spin-Weighted Spherical Harmonics Enable Complete and Scalable $\mathrm{E}(3)$-Equivariant Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01408)
*Chenxing Liang,Yuchao Lin,Andrii Kryvenko,Wendi Yu,Chuan Li,Jianwen Xie,Xiaofeng Qian,Shuiwang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpinGTP通过将Gaunt张量积从标量函数推广到自旋加权球谐函数，恢复了缺失的反对称交互路径，在保持O(L^6)复杂度降低的同时实现了与完整CGTP相当的精度，尤其在手性材料和非中心对称几何任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: E(3)-等变网络的Clebsch-Gordan张量积(CGTP)具有O(L^6)复杂度，限制了可扩展性；而近期提出的Gaunt张量积(GTP)虽降低了复杂度，但无法捕获反对称路径，导致表达能力不完整。

Method: 将GTP从标量函数泛化到自旋加权球谐函数(SWSH)，利用SWSH的代数性质恢复缺失的反对称交互，同时保持GTP的渐进效率。

Result: 在Tetris、3BPA、SPICE-MACE-OFF和OC20等基准测试中，SpinGTP达到了与完整CGTP相当的精度，并且在手性材料和非中心对称几何任务中表现更优。

Conclusion: SpinGTP提供了一条完整、可扩展且数学严谨的路径，可用于大规模3D原子系统模拟中的高阶等变性。

Abstract: $\mathrm{E}(3)$-equivariant networks are promising for 3D atomistic system modeling, yet their scalability is limited by the $O(L^6)$ complexity of the Clebsch-Gordan Tensor Product (CGTP). The recently proposed Gaunt Tensor Product (GTP) reduces the complexity but is unable to capture the antisymmetric paths, resulting in incomplete expressivity. In this work, we present SpinGTP, an approach to overcome the GTP incompleteness by generalizing from scalar functions to Spin-Weighted Spherical Harmonics (SWSH). By relying on the algebraic properties of SWSH, SpinGTP recovers the missing antisymmetric interactions while maintaining the asymptotic efficiency of GTP. It also allows for a more expressive equivariant basis that naturally accounts for the parity-odd components of tensor products. We evaluate SpinGTP across diverse benchmarks, including Tetris, 3BPA, SPICE-MACE-OFF, and OC20. Our results show that SpinGTP achieves accuracies comparable to full CGTP. Notably, by explicitly capturing antisymmetric paths, SpinGTP exhibits superior performance in tasks involving chiral materials and non-centrosymmetric geometries. This work provides a complete, scalable, and mathematically rigorous path toward high-order equivariance in large-scale 3D atomistic system simulations.

</details>


### [72] [The Rollout Infrastructure Tax in Coding-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01415)
*Daniel Thi Graviet,Lovre Pesut,Ivan Dagelic,Vedran Jukic,Ivan Burazin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了四种执行环境（单容器、托管沙箱、Kubernetes容器、云虚拟机）在编码智能体强化学习后训练中的性能差异，发现冷启动延迟相差高达110倍，处理百万条150步轨迹的工时相差1.8倍，主张将执行基础设施作为训练系统的一部分进行优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前编码智能体的强化学习系统将执行基础设施视为背景实现细节，忽略了基础设施开销在大规模交互式软件回滚中带来的效率损失。

Method: 对四种执行环境（单容器、托管沙箱、Kubernetes编排容器、云虚拟机）进行对比研究，测量冷启动延迟和规模化训练工时。

Result: 冷启动延迟差异高达110倍；处理100万条150步轨迹，项目工时差异达1.8倍。

Conclusion: 未来的编码智能体RL系统应将执行子系统的优化纳入训练系统设计本身，而非仅将其视为部署层面的管道问题。

Abstract: Coding-agent reinforcement learning treats execution infrastructure as a background implementation detail, despite relying on large numbers of interactive software rollouts. This is a missed opportunity: measuring infrastructure overhead can reveal practical efficiency gains for RL post-training, where small per-rollout savings compound at scale. We present a comparative study of four execution substrates: single containers, hosted sandboxes, Kubernetes-orchestrated containers, and cloud virtual machines. We find up to $110\times$ variation in cold-start latency and a $1.8\times$ spread in projected worker-hours for one million 150-step trajectories. Our results suggest that future coding-agent RL systems should optimize execution substrates as part of the training system itself, not merely as deployment plumbing.

</details>


### [73] [Conditional Inference Trees and Forests for Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01417)
*Robert Milletich,Justin Downes,Steve Goley,Newel Hirst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了条件推断树(CIT)和条件推断森林(CIF)作为top-k特征排序方法在下游预测任务中的表现，发现CIF在分类和回归基准上分别排名第4和第3，并通过运行时消融实验揭示了自适应停止和阈值搜索数量对运行时间影响最大，同时指出森林特征采样可能遗漏重要特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件推断树和条件推断森林通过测试特征后再选择分割阈值来减少分割选择偏差，但重复的置换检验和阈值搜索导致计算开销巨大，因此需要系统评估其在特征排序和下游预测中的实际表现与计算效率。

Method: 在固定节点上，如果特征和置换预算不依赖于节点响应，则Bonferroni校正的+1蒙特卡洛置换p值在完全置换零假设下控制节点级拒绝率。通过真实数据基准、运行时消融实验和合成特征恢复实验进行评估，其中CIF在17种分类方法（22个数据集）和18种回归方法（8个数据集）中进行排名比较。

Result: CIF在22个数据集的17种分类方法中排名第4，在8个数据集的18种回归方法中排名第3。运行时消融实验显示：关闭自适应停止和使用精确阈值搜索分别使拟合时间增加4.0-8.4倍和1.9-10.8倍，而下游评分变化最多为0.011。稀疏高维模拟表明森林特征采样可能使重要特征被排除在许多分割决策之外。

Conclusion: 结果支持CIF作为在评估的下游预测基准中有效的top-k特征排序方法。

Abstract: Conditional inference trees (CIT) and conditional inference forests (CIF) reduce split-selection bias by testing features before choosing split thresholds, but repeated permutation tests and threshold searches can make these methods computationally expensive. We study CIT and CIF as top-$k$ feature-ranking methods for downstream prediction using real-data benchmarks, runtime ablations, and synthetic feature-recovery experiments. At a fixed node, if the features and permutation budget do not depend on the node responses, Bonferroni-corrected $+1$ Monte Carlo permutation $p$-values control nodewise rejection under the complete permutation null. CIF ranks 4th among 17 classification methods on 22 datasets and 3rd among 18 regression methods on 8 datasets. With Bonferroni correction held fixed, the CIF runtime ablations indicate that adaptive stopping and the number of thresholds searched have the largest measured effect on runtime: turning off adaptive stopping and using exact threshold search increase fitting time by 4.0--8.4$\times$ and 1.9--10.8$\times$, respectively, while downstream score changes are at most 0.011. Sparse high-$p$ simulations indicate that forest feature sampling can leave informative features out of many split decisions. Overall, the results support CIF as a top-$k$ feature-ranking method in the evaluated downstream prediction benchmarks.

</details>


### [74] [On the Utility and Factual Reliability of Pruned Mixture-of-Experts Models in the Biomedical Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01444)
*Atsuki Yamaguchi,Szymon Palucha,Léo Bijar,Aline Villavicencio,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了MoE模型中结构化专家剪枝对事实可靠性的影响，发现在中等剪枝率下领域内效用保持但极端剪枝导致幻觉风险增加，跨领域时效用与可靠性均快速下降，表明仅评估效用不足以保障高安全场景部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要评估剪枝对基准效用的影响，忽略了事实可靠性的变化，尤其在生物医学等高风险领域缺乏探索。

Method: 评估了四种MoE模型、六种剪枝方法及多种剪枝比例，在领域内（生物医学）和跨领域设置下，针对生成任务和分类任务进行实验。

Result: 中等剪枝率可保持领域内效用且可靠性不立即下降，但极端剪枝率下幻觉风险增加；跨领域时效用与可靠性均快速退化。

Conclusion: 安全压缩高度依赖于任务和领域，仅凭效用评估不足以判断剪枝后MoE模型在高安全场景中的部署可行性，必须纳入可靠性评估。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models offer inference speedups via selective activation but impose substantial memory requirements because the whole network must remain loaded. Structured expert pruning is a practical approach for reducing deployment costs in resource-constrained settings. However, prior studies primarily evaluate benchmark utility, leaving the effect of pruning on factual reliability underexplored, particularly in high-stakes domains such as biomedicine. In this paper, we investigate how domain-specific expert pruning affects both utility and reliability. We assess four MoE models, six pruning methods, and multiple pruning ratios across generation and classification tasks under in-domain (biomedical) and cross-domain settings. Results reveal that moderate pruning preserves in-domain utility without immediate reliability decline, although hallucination risks increase at extreme pruning ratios. When shifting to the general domain, both utility and reliability degrade rapidly. These findings indicate that safe compression depends heavily on the task and domain. Evaluating pruned MoE models solely on utility is inadequate for high-stakes deployment without reliability assessment.

</details>


### [75] [Geometry-Aware R-Structured Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01449)
*Sergei Kucherenko,Nilay Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种几何感知的R-结构KAN网络(GRS-KAN)，通过将Rvachev的R函数集成到KAN框架中，实现了平滑非线性学习与几何/逻辑约束的显式编码相结合，在含间断的回归问题上相比标准KAN降低了67%的测试RMSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准KAN网络缺乏显式编码几何约束和逻辑结构的能力，难以处理包含间断、可行区域和隐式几何边界的回归问题，亟需一种能融合可微逻辑运算与神经网络的混合架构。

Method: 将Rvachev的R函数（R-合取与R-析取）作为可微逻辑运算引入KAN框架，通过R函数编码几何/逻辑约束，KAN分支学习平滑非线性结构；提出了加性、乘性和不可知分支加权三种变体架构。

Result: 在含圆形和矩形支撑间断的回归基准上，GRS-KAN相比标准KAN降低测试RMSE最高达67%，边界定位精度显著提升；不可知变体能自动判断几何先验是否有益。

Conclusion: 显式几何编码能显著提升预测精度和可解释性，GRS-KAN为融合先验几何知识与数据驱动学习的可微神经网络提供了一种有效框架。

Abstract: We propose a novel hybrid neural architecture, the Geometry-aware R-Structured Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (GRS-KAN), which integrates V.L.Rvachev's R-functions into the Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (KAN) framework. The proposed approach combines two complementary modeling mechanisms: smooth nonlinear structure is learned by KAN branches, while known geometric or logical constraints are encoded analytically using differentiable R-functions. This enables explicit representation of discontinuities, feasible regions, and implicit geometric boundaries within a trainable neural architecture.
  The framework implements differentiable logical operations through R-conjunctions and R-disjunctions, allowing complex geometric supports to be represented analytically and incorporated directly into regression models. Several GRS-KAN variants are introduced, including additive, multiplicative, and agnostic branch-weighted architectures.
  The method is demonstrated on regression problems involving discontinuities with circular and rectangular supports. Numerical experiments show that explicit geometric encoding substantially improves predictive accuracy and boundary localization compared with standard KANs. In the considered benchmarks, geometry-aware GRS-KAN models reduce test RMSE by up to 67% while simultaneously improving interpretability through explicit analytical representation of the learned geometric structure. The agnostic variant further demonstrates the ability to automatically determine whether geometric priors are beneficial for a given learning task.

</details>


### [76] [Token Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01455)
*Kathan Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Ember优化器，专门针对语言模型的embedding和LM-head矩阵，利用其梯度几何特性，用仅KB级优化器状态实现优于Adam的帕累托边界，并揭示了token优化轨迹可被一维射线描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的embedding表和LM-head头作为离散符号与连续程序之间的读写接口，其梯度几何特性与密集隐藏权重不同，现有Adam优化器对此类参数需要O(2VD)的优化器状态，效率低下。

Method: 提出Ember优化器，针对embedding和LM-head矩阵设计轻量级优化方案，仅需O(V + D)显存（V为词表大小，D为隐藏维度），避免了Adam中token表优化器状态的分片需求。通过理论分析和实验验证其梯度轨迹可被简单1D射线描述。

Result: Ember在监督微调、强化学习和预训练中均能改善帕累托边界；有效扩展到不同batch size和参数量；揭示了token优化轨迹的1D射线特性，挑战了神经网络参数处于高度非凸景观的传统观点。

Conclusion: 嵌入层和LM-head的优化存在特殊几何结构，Ember利用这一特性实现了高效的轻量级优化，对Transformer训练中优化器的选择空间提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Language models learn continuous programs over discrete symbols, with the embedding table and LM-head acting as the read/write interface between them. We show that this interface has gradient geometry distinct from dense hidden weights which can be exploited to improve the Pareto frontier across supervised finetuning, RL, and pretraining, while only utilizing kilobytes of optimizer state. We introduce Ember, a lightweight optimizer for embedding and LM-head matrices that utilizes O(V + D) VRAM, instead of Adam's O(2VD), and forgoes the need to shard both token table optimizer states. We provide empirical evidence that Ember scales effectively across batch size and parameter count. We show that the optimization trajectory of tokens can be well described by a simple 1D ray, counter to the popular belief that neural net parameters navigate a heavily nonconvex landscape. We provide a principled view on the surprisingly narrow space of optimizers that suffice for Transformer training. Finally, we open-source our distributed Ember implementation that merges cleanly with existing ZeRO/FSDP setups to support further research at https://github.com/katop1234/ember

</details>


### [77] [Class-Grouped Normalized Momentum and Faster Hyperparameter Exploration to Tackle Class Imbalance in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01474)
*Haemin Park,Diego Klabjan,Martin W. Braun,Xiuqi Li,Balakrishnan Ananthanarayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了联邦学习中处理类别不平衡的客户端优化器FedCGNM（基于类分组归一化动量）和超参数优化算法FedHOO（基于X-armed-bandit），在多个长尾数据集上取得一致优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中的类别不平衡问题导致少数类预测性能差，且由于隐私和异构性约束，标准集中式技术无法直接应用。

Method: FedCGNM将类别按最小组内方差分组成少量组，为每组维护动量并归一化为单位长度，求和后作为更新方向；FedHOO利用X-armed-bandit在小型客户端场景下高效优化重采样率。

Result: 在四个公开长尾基准数据集和一个专有芯片缺陷数据集上，FedCGNM一致优于基线方法，FedHOO在小规模联邦场景中带来进一步提升。

Conclusion: 通过类分组归一化动量平衡多数类和少数类梯度幅度并抑制噪声，结合XAB超参优化，有效提升了联邦学习在不平衡数据下的表现。

Abstract: Class imbalance poses a critical challenge in federated learning (FL), where underrepresented classes suffer from poor predictive performance yet cannot be addressed by standard centralized techniques due to privacy and heterogeneity constraints. We propose FedCGNM (Federated Class-Grouped Normalized Momentum), a client-side optimizer in FL that partitions classes into a small number of groups based on minimum within-group variance, maintains a momentum per group, normalizes each group momentum to unit length, and uses the summation of the normalized group momentums as an update direction. This design both equalizes gradient magnitude across majority and minority groups and mitigates the noise inherent in rare-class gradients. We further provide a theoretical convergence analysis explicitly accounting for time-varying resampling-rates.
