Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed devices without sharing raw data, making it suitable for privacy-sensitive robotic sensing applications. However, multi-agent systems generate heterogeneous and non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) multimodal sensor streams that degrade conventional FL algorithms, while classical fusion modules introduce substantial parameter overhead and communication cost. This paper proposes QFedAgent, a hybrid quantum-classical personalized FL framework for multi-agent activity recognition. The approach integrates a variational quantum circuit fusion module that models accelerometer--gyroscope interactions through quantum state encoding and entanglement, requiring only 72 quantum rotation parameters versus 33K in classical multi-layer perceptron-based fusion, achieving approximately 10x total parameter reduction. Experiments on the OPPORTUNITY dataset under subject-based non-IID partitions demonstrate 97.7% mean test accuracy, confirming that parameter-efficient quantum fusion remains competitive with conventional federated baselines.

</details>


### [152] [Extreme Adaptive Transformer for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02437)
*Sanjeev Shrestha,Hui Liu,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出极端自适应Transformer（Exformer），通过包含局部、步长和极端三种稀疏注意力组件，显式建模正常与极端事件的时间依赖关系，在四个水文数据集上实现更优的3天预报性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列中罕见但关键的极端事件常被忽视，尤其在洪水预报等水文领域，现有Transformer模型对所有时间点统一处理，难以充分表征极端模式。

Method: 提出Exformer框架，核心是极端自适应注意力机制，包含三个稀疏组件：Local（捕捉短期依赖）、Stride（捕捉周期依赖）、Extreme（选择性建模正常与极端事件之间的感知依赖）。

Result: 在四个真实水文径流数据集上，Exformer在3天预报任务中优于现有基线模型。

Conclusion: 显式纳入极端感知注意力能提升Transformer在不平衡时间序列（含罕见但重要事件）上的预报能力。

Abstract: Time series forecasting remains challenging when the underlying data contain rare but critical extreme events. This issue is particularly important in hydrologic forecasting, where streamflow distributions are often highly skewed and extreme peaks can have substantial impacts on flood monitoring, water resource management, and early warning systems. Although Transformer-based forecasting models have achieved strong performance by modeling long-range temporal dependencies, they typically treat all time points uniformly and may therefore underrepresent rare extreme patterns. In this paper, we propose the Extreme-Adaptive Transformer (Exformer), a forecasting framework designed to explicitly model temporal dependencies involving both normal and extreme events. Exformer introduces an extreme-adaptive attention mechanism composed of three sparse components: Local, Stride, and Extreme. The Local and Stride components capture short-term and periodic temporal dependencies, respectively, while the Extreme component selectively models event-aware dependencies between normal and extreme streamflow patterns. Experiments on four real-world hydrologic streamflow datasets show that Exformer achieves superior 3-day forecasting performance compared with state-of-the-art baselines. Our findings demonstrate that explicitly incorporating extreme-aware attention improves the forecasting capacity of Transformer models on imbalanced time series with rare but consequential events.

</details>


### [153] [Understanding the Robustness of Distributed Self-Supervised Learning Frameworks Against Non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02447)
*Xuanyu Chen,Nan Yang,Shuai Wang,Dong Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对分布式自监督学习（D-SSL）在非独立同分布（non-IID）数据下的鲁棒性进行了严格理论分析，发现掩码图像建模（MIM）在异构数据下比对比学习（CL）更鲁棒，且联邦学习（FL）的鲁棒性不低于去中心化学习（DecL）。基于理论发现，提出MAR损失函数，实验验证了理论并证实了MAR损失的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式自监督学习（D-SSL）面临数据异构性的关键挑战，但现有研究缺乏对不同D-SSL框架在异构数据下鲁棒性的理论理解。

Method: 对D-SSL框架在non-IID设置下进行严格理论分析，比较掩码图像建模（MIM）与对比学习（CL）的鲁棒性，分析平均网络连通性的影响，并基于理论设计MAR损失（带局部到全局对齐正则化的MIM目标改进）。

Result: MIM预训练在异构数据下比CL更鲁棒；D-SSL的鲁棒性随平均网络连通性增加而提高，FL的鲁棒性不低于DecL；MAR损失在多种模型架构和分布式设置下有效。

Conclusion: 理论分析为未来D-SSL算法设计提供了坚实的理论基础，MAR损失作为理论的应用实例进一步验证了理论发现。

Abstract: Recent research has introduced distributed self-supervised learning (D-SSL) approaches to leverage vast amounts of unlabeled decentralized data. However, D-SSL faces the critical challenge of data heterogeneity, and there is limited theoretical understanding of how different D-SSL frameworks respond to this challenge. To fill this gap, we present a rigorous theoretical analysis of the robustness of D-SSL frameworks under non-IID (non-independent and identically distributed) settings. Our results show that pre-training with Masked Image Modeling (MIM) is inherently more robust to heterogeneous data than Contrastive Learning (CL), and that the robustness of decentralized SSL increases with average network connectivity, implying that federated learning (FL) is no less robust than decentralized learning (DecL). These findings provide a solid theoretical foundation for guiding the design of future D-SSL algorithms. To further illustrate the practical implications of our theory, we introduce MAR loss, a refinement of the MIM objective with local-to-global alignment regularization. Extensive experiments across model architectures and distributed settings validate our theoretical insights, and additionally confirm the effectiveness of MAR loss as an application of our analysis.

</details>


### [154] [Neuron-Aware Data Selection for Annotation-Free LLM Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02460)
*Zhuowei Chen,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Neuron-OPSD框架，利用神经元激活指导训练数据选择和教师上下文构建，通过在线策略自蒸馏实现无标注LLM后训练，在提升领域内性能的同时保持跨域泛化并缓解校准误差膨胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无标注自进化方法（SFT/GRPO变体、基于奖励的在线策略RL）存在域外性能下降和校准误差膨胀问题，且领域专家标注成本高昂，亟需一种无需人工标注或真实交互反馈的模型后训练方法。

Method: 提出Neuron-OPSD框架：(1) 利用内部神经元激活值指导训练数据选择和教师上下文构建；(2) 通过在线策略蒸馏从教师分布中训练模型，全程无需真实标签。

Result: 在专业领域基准上，Neuron-OPSD提升了领域内任务性能，保持了跨域泛化能力，并缓解了校准误差膨胀，优于现有无标注基线方法。

Conclusion: 该框架适用于在线交互或外部监督成本高昂/不可行的场景，且与依赖已标注奖励轨迹的离线RL方法有本质区别，为无标注自蒸馏提供了有效的数据中心化解决方案。

Abstract: Post-training large language models (LLMs) without real-world interaction feedback or human-labeled supervision remains challenging, particularly in specialized domains where expert annotations are costly to obtain. Recent annotation-free self-evolution methods address this by using the model's own outputs as supervision signals, constructing a teacher via additional context and aggregating predictions across multiple rollouts through majority voting to produce pseudo-labels. However, these approaches are not without drawbacks: SFT- and GRPO-based variants suffer out-of-domain performance degradation, while reward-based on-policy RL inflates calibration error. In this paper, we propose Neuron On-Policy Self-Distillation (Neuron-OPSD), a data-centric framework for annotation-free self-distillation that leverages internal neuron activations to guide both training-data selection and teacher context construction. The model is then trained via on-policy distillation from the teacher distribution, requiring no ground-truth labels at any stage. Across specialized-domain benchmarks, Neuron-OPSD improves in-domain task performance while preserving cross-domain generalization and mitigating calibration collapse over prior annotation-free baselines. This framework is particularly relevant to settings where online interaction or external supervision is costly or infeasible, and is conceptually distinct from offline RL approaches that rely on logged, reward-labeled trajectories.

</details>


### [155] [Beyond Adam: SOAP and Muon for Faster, Label-Efficient Training of Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02499)
*Gil Harari,Yoel Zimmermann,Ola Tangen Kulseng,Laura Zichi,Chuin Wei Tan,Marc L. Descoteaux,Boris Kozinsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了矩阵结构优化器（Muon、SOAP、SOAP-Muon）与Adam在训练NequIP和Allegro MLIP模型时的性能，发现这些新型优化器在收敛速度和最终精度上显著优于Adam，其中SOAP和SOAP-Muon表现最为稳健，特别是在部分力监督条件下优势更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIP已成为AI科学模拟的标志性方法，但训练优化器的选择在社区中一直被忽视，默认使用Adam及其变体，缺乏对更优优化器的系统性探索。

Method: 实现并系统比较了最近提出的矩阵结构优化器（Muon、SOAP及混合SOAP-Muon）与Adam在NequIP和Allegro两种MLIP模型上的训练性能。

Result: SOAP和SOAP-Muon在收敛速度和最终精度上均显著优于Adam，且表现稳健；Muon仅带来部分提升；在部分力监督条件下改进尤为明显。

Conclusion: 优化器选择是MLIP训练中被忽视但影响巨大的设计维度，矩阵结构优化器有望成为替代Adam的更优选择。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have become a hallmark of AI for scientific simulation. While efforts on new architectures and datasets have led to increasingly accurate and general models, the choice of optimizer for training has largely remained unexplored, defaulting to Adam and its variants in the community. Here, we implement and systematically compare a class of recently proposed matrix-structured optimizers, including Muon, SOAP, and the hybrid SOAP-Muon, for training NequIP and Allegro MLIP models. We find that these optimizers can substantially outperform Adam in both convergence speed and final accuracy. SOAP and SOAP-Muon emerge as robust and consistently strong methods, while Muon only provides partial gains relative to Adam. The improvements are particularly pronounced under partial force supervision. Our results indicate that optimizer choice is an overlooked yet impactful design axis for MLIPs.

</details>


### [156] [DemoPSD: Disagreement-Modulated Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02502)
*Yunhe Li,Hao Shi,Wenhao Liu,Mengzhe Ruan,Hanxu Hou,Zhongxiang Dai,Shuang Qiu,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DemoPSD通过选择性采纳教师引导，构建逆向KL重心目标来平衡教师与学生分布，从而解决特权信息泄露和探索性退化问题，在科学推理任务上优于GRPO和SDPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPSD方法中，教师基于特权信息提供密集token级监督，导致学生过拟合领域内模式、抑制探索、损害跨域泛化，且存在更根本的特权信息泄露问题——学生编码了测试时不可用的答案依赖捷径。

Method: 提出DemoPSD框架，核心思想是选择性采纳教师指导：构建逆向KL重心目标（教师与学生分布的加权几何组合），利用分布差异自适应控制每个token位置的混合比例，在拟合教师分布与保持自身推理能力之间取得平衡。

Result: 在SciKnowEval四个科学领域上，DemoPSD优于GRPO和SDPO；保持更高的训练熵；在OOD的GPQA基准上展现出鲁棒的泛化能力。

Conclusion: DemoPSD从理论上证明了可以实现泄露衰减（有效缓解特权信息泄露）和探索保持（在密集token级蒸馏下保持探索能力），为OPSD提供了更稳健的训练范式。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) has emerged as a practical method for training large language models (LLMs) to reason, where a single model acts as both the teacher and the student with different levels of information access. However, recent studies have found that the teacher's dense token-level supervision, conditioned on privileged information, can lead to overfitting to in-domain patterns, suppress exploration, and hurt cross-domain generalization, while also introducing a more fundamental issue: *privileged information leakage*, where the student encodes answer-dependent shortcuts that are unavailable at test time. We introduce **DemoPSD**, a novel framework that resolves such problems through the idea of *selective adoption of teacher guidance*. Instead of fitting the full teacher distribution, DemoPSD steers the student toward a *reverse-KL barycenter target*, a weighted geometric combination of the teacher and student distributions, that naturally balances learning from the teacher with preserving the student's own reasoning capacity. We measure the difference between their distributions and use such a discrepancy to adaptively control the blending at each token position. We provably show that DemoPSD achieves **(1)** *leakage attenuation*, i.e., effective mitigation of privileged information leakage; and **(2)** *exploration preservation*, i.e., preservation of exploration capacity under dense token-level distillation. Extensive experiments on SciKnowEval across four scientific fields show that DemoPSD outperforms both GRPO and SDPO while maintaining higher training entropy and robustly generalizing to out-of-distribution GPQA benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Bilinear control of age--space structured populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01347)
*Jiguang Yu,Louis Shuo Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究具有内生反馈的非局部年龄-空间结构种群方程的双线性最优控制问题，通过特征线温和公式建立了闭环适定性和Frechet可微性，推导了反馈校正的伴随方程，并证明了最优性条件中切换函数的分解结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在种群动态控制中，控制不仅影响系统动力学，还通过观测反馈影响边界条件，形成非线性闭环映射，传统Lions-Magenes方法难以处理此类反馈依赖的非光滑结构，需要新的分析框架。

Method: 采用特征线温和公式替代标准Lions-Magenes论证，处理反馈依赖的非线性闭环控制-状态映射；将反馈导数识别为低秩扰动项$\ell_{\bar y,\bar u}(p)(t)χ(a,x)$，并利用Volterra核下转移算子的拟幂零性得到伴随的显式预解表示。

Result: 建立了闭环系统的适定性和Frechet可微性；推导了约化伴随方程和反馈校正伴随方程；证明了切换函数可分解为约化分量和反馈诱导分量。

Conclusion: 该工作为带内生反馈的非局部种群方程最优控制提供了完整的理论基础，其低秩扰动结构和拟幂零算子方法可推广至其他反馈控制系统。

Abstract: We study constrained bilinear optimal control for nonlocal age--space structured population equations with renewal boundary conditions and endogenous surveillance feedback. The control acts as a coefficient in a mixed transport--diffusion equation, while a scalar observable generated by the state enters both the interior dynamics and the renewal law. This produces a nonlinear closed-loop control-to-state map and a feedback-dependent adjoint system. Using a characteristic mild formulation rather than a standard Lions--Magenes argument, we establish closed-loop well-posedness and Frechet differentiability. We then derive the reduced and feedback-corrected adjoint equations. The feedback derivative is identified as a low-rank perturbation $\ell_{\bar y,\bar u}(p)(t)χ(a,x)$; in the Volterra-kernel regime, the associated transfer operator is quasinilpotent, yielding an explicit resolvent representation of the adjoint. Finally, we prove first-order optimality conditions and decompose the switching function into reduced and feedback-induced components.

</details>


### [158] [Flexible and Reliable Network Design for Emerging Transportation Services: Multi-stage Stochastic Programming Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01524)
*Riki Kawase,Koki Satsukawa,Toru Seo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种灵活且可靠网络设计问题（FR-NDPs）的通用框架，将战略投资决策与运营控制相结合，构建了可通过随机对偶动态规划求解的风险规避多阶段随机模型，并在曼哈顿中城网络上验证了灵活性与可靠性在共享自动驾驶汽车和快速公交系统规划中的互补作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴交通服务（如共享自动驾驶汽车系统）面临历史数据稀缺和技术发展不确定性的挑战，现有网络设计方法难以在灵活性和可靠性之间取得平衡，亟需一种能够动态调整基础设施投资并保证可靠性的通用框架。

Method: 将FR-NDPs建模为风险规避多阶段随机规划问题，采用随机对偶动态规划（SDDP）求解，并建立了战略子问题和运营子问题收敛到全局最优的充分条件。通过SAV容量扩展和SAV-BRT综合路线设计两个应用案例进行验证。

Result: 在曼哈顿中城网络上的数值实验表明：（i）灵活性和可靠性具有互补效应，可对冲严重情景同时减少预期性能损失；（ii）投资规划的灵活性允许动态风险对冲，风险规避型规划者倾向于减少早期投资以保持适应性；（iii）SAV与BRT系统在运营灵活性上的差异会反映在战略决策中，风险规避型规划者倾向于避免对运营灵活性较低的交通模式进行投资。

Conclusion: 灵活性和可靠性在网络设计中具有互补性，所提框架能有效指导不确定环境下新兴交通系统的基础设施投资决策，风险偏好显著影响投资时机和模式选择。

Abstract: This paper proposes a general framework for flexible and reliable network design problems (FR-NDPs). The framework enables planners to change infrastructure investments in response to realized uncertainties, while ensuring desired levels of reliability. Motivated by emerging transportation services such as shared autonomous vehicle (SAV) systems, where historical data are scarce and technological developments uncertain, FR-NDPs integrate strategic investment decisions with operational control. We formulate the FR-NDPs as risk-averse multi-stage stochastic problems to be solvable by stochastic dual dynamic programming (SDDP) and establish sufficient conditions under which strategic and operational subproblems converge to the global optimum. We illustrate applications to SAV capacity expansion and integrated SAV-BRT (Bus Rapid Transit) route design, and numerical experiments on a Midtown Manhattan network highlight three key findings: (i) flexibility and reliability act complementarily to hedge against severe scenarios while mitigating the loss of expected performance; (ii) flexibility in investment planning allows dynamic risk hedging, with risk-averse planners reducing early-stage investments to preserve adaptability; and (iii) differences in operational flexibility between SAV and BRT systems are reflected in strategic decisions, with risk-averse planners tending to refrain investment in transport modes with lower operational flexibility.

</details>


### [159] [Mean Field Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01525)
*René Carmona,Mathieu Laurière*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过马尔可夫决策过程的视角，系统介绍了平均场强化学习的基本理论框架，涵盖从多智能体强化学习到平均场控制的连接、概率与控制理论基础、代表性智能体学习问题、有限种群系统的关系分析、动态规划原理、混沌传播极限、表格型Q学习和策略梯度方法的理论分析，以及数值实现方法（包括表格方案和深度确定性策略梯度等深度强化学习方法）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为大规模随机种群控制问题提供从平均场控制理论到强化学习方法论的连贯桥梁，强调问题的数学结构并设计可行的学习策略。

Method: 以马尔可夫决策过程为核心，结合平均场交互与公共噪声，建立概率论、数学和控制论框架；研究一般模型与线性二次模型；采用动态规划原理、混沌传播极限、表格型Q学习和策略梯度方法进行理论分析；数值实现涵盖表格方案和深度确定性策略梯度等深度强化学习方法。

Result: 建立了平均场控制理论与强化学习之间的系统性桥梁，给出了代表性智能体学习问题的数学框架及其与有限种群系统的关系，完成了相关算法的理论分析与数值实现方法验证。

Conclusion: 平均场强化学习通过将大规模多智能体问题转化为代表性智能体的学习问题，提供了处理大型随机种群控制的有效数学框架与计算方法。

Abstract: This monograph provides an introduction to mean field reinforcement learning through the lens of Markov decision processes arising from large-population stochastic control with mean field interactions and common noise. Starting from the connection between multi-agent reinforcement learning and mean field control, it develops the probabilistic, mathematical, and control-theoretic framework needed to formulate representative-agent learning problems, analyze their relationship with finite-population systems, and study both general and linear-quadratic models. The presentation includes dynamic programming principles, propagation-of-chaos limits, and theoretical analyses of tabular Q-learning and policy-gradient methods. It also discusses numerical implementations, including tabular schemes and deep reinforcement learning methods such as deep deterministic policy gradient. The goal is to give readers a coherent bridge between mean field control theory and reinforcement learning methodology, emphasizing the mathematical structure of the problems and the design of tractable learning approaches for large stochastic populations.

</details>


### [160] [Multi-Domain Matrix Framework for Human Resource Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01613)
*Daehan Lee,Jaemyung Ahn,Jaewoo Kim*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多域矩阵（MDM）的小企业和初创企业人力资源决策支持框架，通过将成员、技能和项目建模为互联域，提供定性分析指南和定量指标来诊断组织HR状态并支持工作负载再分配、招聘和能力开发等决策，案例研究验证了其实际应用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型组织面临三个关键挑战：组织组件之间复杂的相互依赖关系、缺乏系统性的HR决策分析工具、以及快速变化环境中需要快速响应。现有HR方法缺乏针对这些小企业特有需求的系统性工具。

Method: 将初创企业人力资源管理形式化为多域结构建模问题，使用MDM集成成员、技能和项目三个相互关联的域；基于该表示提供定性分析指南和定量指标用于诊断HR状态；通过早期技术初创企业的案例研究进行验证。

Result: 案例研究显示框架能够识别工作负载不平衡，揭示关键成员不可持续的工作负载，并为后续招聘决策提供信息；招聘新成员后可继续应用框架跟踪组织多域结构变化并支持持续HR诊断。

Conclusion: 该MDM框架为小企业和初创企业提供了一种系统化的HR决策支持方法，能够有效应对组织组件复杂依赖、缺乏分析工具和需要快速响应等挑战，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: This paper presents an actionable human resource (HR) decision-support framework for small firms and startups based on a multi-domain matrix (MDM). The framework addresses three key challenges faced by small organizations: complex interdependencies among organizational components; the lack of systematic analytical tools for HR decision-making; and the need for rapid responses in fast-changing organizational environments. The proposed framework formulates startup human resource management as a multi-domain structural modeling problem, where members, skills, and projects are interconnected domains within an integrated MDM. Based on this representation, the framework provides qualitative analysis guidelines and quantitative metrics for diagnosing an organization's HR state and supporting personnel decisions on workload redistribution, hiring, and capability development. A case study of MDM-based HR decisions for an early-stage technology startup is conducted to demonstrate the framework's practical applicability. The application shows that the framework can identify workload imbalances, reveal a key member with an unsustainable workload, and inform a subsequent hiring decision. The framework can be further applied after the hiring of a new member to track changes in the organization's multi-domain structure and support continuous HR diagnosis.

</details>


### [161] [Decentralized Stochastic Subgradient-type Methods with Communication Compression for Nonsmooth Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01755)
*Siyuan Zhang,Nachuan Xiao,Xin Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对非光滑非凸分布式优化问题，提出了一个统一框架，涵盖带无偏压缩和带误差补偿的收缩压缩的分布式随机次梯度方法。通过将共识误差和平均迭代轨迹与连续时间微分包含相关联，在目标函数非光滑且不满足Clarke正则性时，建立了框架内所有方法的全局收敛性。基于该框架，进一步开发了多种基于压缩的方法，实验验证了理论结果并展示了通信-精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式优化方法多假设目标函数光滑或满足Clarke正则性，且缺乏对非光滑非凸场景下压缩通信的统一理论分析框架。

Method: 提出统一框架，涵盖无偏压缩和带误差补偿的收缩压缩；通过连续时间微分包含轨迹关联共识误差与平均迭代；基于框架开发了基于符号正则化和梯度跟踪动量的分布式随机次梯度方法。

Result: 建立了非光滑非凸且不满足Clarke正则性条件下的全局收敛性；数值实验验证了理论结果并揭示了通信-精度权衡。

Conclusion: 所提统一框架能有效分析多种压缩通信下的分布式非光滑非凸优化方法，新开发的方法在通信效率和精度间实现了可调节的平衡。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the nonsmooth nonconvex decentralized optimization problem, where inter-agent communication is compressed. We propose a general framework that unifies various decentralized stochastic subgradient-type methods with unbiased compression and contractive compression with error compensation. By relating the consensus-error iterates and the averaged iterates to the trajectories of continuous-time differential inclusions, we establish global convergence for all methods encompassed by our framework when the objective functions are nonsmooth and lack Clarke regularity. Based on our framework, we further develop several compression-based methods, including decentralized stochastic subgradient methods utilizing sign-based regularization and gradient-tracking momentum. Preliminary numerical experiments empirically support our theoretical results and highlight the communication-accuracy trade-off of the newly developed methods.

</details>


### [162] [Cognitive Warfare, Hybrid Pressure, and Sovereign Resilience: An Operations Research Framework Applied to the Iranian Case (2017--2026)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01894)
*Rahimeh Neamatian Monemi,Shahin Gelareh,Nelson Maculan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对对手持续优化认知战役下防御方的动态响应问题，提出一个耦合动力系统模型，将 grievance（不满情绪）与 institutional resilience（制度韧性）建模为连续变量，压力强度由马尔可夫决策过程优化选择。证明均衡的存在性与局部稳定性，并通过伊朗认知战案例（2017-2026）和30个随机网络实例验证。案例显示距计算出的运作崩溃边界约25倍，贪婪播种策略网络渗透率达87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运筹学中的攻防模型将防御方状态视为离散分配，无法刻画防御方作为连续、缓慢变化的制度过程，无法直接解决防御方在持续经济、媒体和心理压力下的动态响应问题。

Method: 构建耦合动力系统：不满情绪与制度韧性连续演化，压力强度由优化马尔可夫决策过程选择；证明均衡存在性与局部稳定性；通过30个随机网络实例、完整值迭代和伊朗认知战案例（2017-2026）进行验证。

Result: 伊朗案例距运作崩溃边界约25倍；贪婪播种策略在随机实例中平均网络渗透率达87%，显著高于度中心性基线。

Conclusion: 从业者可通过均衡与边界计算评估特定案例相对于崩溃边界的位置，无需依赖未经验证的压力强度对比。

Abstract: A defending state facing sustained economic, media, and psychological pressure from an adversary that continuously re-optimises its campaign poses a problem that existing attacker-defender models in operations research do not directly resolve, because they treat the defender's state as a discrete allocation rather than a continuous, slow-moving institutional process. We formulate a coupled dynamical system in which grievance and institutional resilience evolve continuously while pressure intensity is chosen by an optimising Markov decision process, prove existence and local stability of the resulting equilibrium, and prove a formal result distinguishing it from standard feedback-stability analysis and from a stationary Markov chain treated in isolation. We validate the framework computationally using thirty randomised network instances, full value iteration, and a documented case study of cognitive warfare directed at Iran (2017--2026). The historically calibrated case sits approximately twenty-five times above the computed operational collapse boundary, and a greedy seeding policy reaches eighty-seven percent average network penetration across the randomised instances, significantly above a degree-centrality baseline. A practitioner can use the equilibrium and boundary computation to assess where a specific case sits relative to collapse, rather than relying on an unverified comparison between opposing pressure intensities.

</details>


### [163] [Lower Bounds for Anytime Acceleration of Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02053)
*Chung-En Tsai,Ilyas Fatkhullin,Liang Zhang,Niao He*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了梯度下降法在平滑凸优化中的任意时间收敛下界，证明函数值最小化无法达到 o(n^{-1.334}) 的任意时间收敛率，平方梯度范数最小化无法达到 o(n^{-1}) 的任意时间收敛率。通过分析二次函数和 Huber 函数的变体，推导出大步长数量和幅度的新上界，首次为 COLT 2024 开放问题提供了下界结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究显示，在已知总迭代次数 n 时，GD 可达 O(n^{-1.271}) 的收敛率；但在任意时间收敛（n 事先未知）场景下，最佳已知率为函数值 O(n^{-1.119}) 和平方梯度范数 O(n^{-1})，远低于经典一阶方法的 Ω(n^{-2}) 下界。这些上界能否改进仍是开放问题。

Method: 通过分析 GD 在二次函数和 Huber 函数变体上的行为，推导出大步长数量和幅度的新上界，从而建立任意时间收敛的下界。

Result: 证明无正步长调度可在函数值最小化中达到 o(n^{-1.334}) 的任意时间收敛率，或在平方梯度范数最小化中达到 o(n^{-1}) 的任意时间收敛率。

Conclusion: 首次为 Kornowski 和 Shamir 在 COLT 2024 提出的关于 GD 最优任意时间收敛率的开放问题提供了下界结果。

Abstract: Recent work suggests that the convergence rate of gradient descent (GD) in smooth convex optimization can be significantly improved by employing large stepsizes that may violate the descent property. In particular, if the total number of iterations $n$ is given, an $O(n^{-1.271})$ convergence rate can be achieved for both function value and squared gradient norm minimization. On the other hand, in the setting of anytime convergence, where $n$ is not known in advance, the best known rates of GD are much slower: $O(n^{-1.119})$ for function value minimization and $O(n^{-1})$ for squared gradient norm minimization. It remains open whether any of these upper bounds can be improved, as they are far from the classical $Ω(n^{-2})$ lower bound for any first-order method.
  In this work, we establish two lower bounds on the anytime convergence of GD. We show that no positive stepsize schedule can achieve an $o(n^{-1.334})$ anytime rate for function value minimization, nor an $o(n^{-1})$ anytime rate for squared gradient norm minimization. The key ingredients of our analysis are novel upper bounds on the number and the magnitude of large stepsizes, derived by analyzing GD on quadratic functions and variants of Huber functions. Our work provides the first lower bounds for the COLT 2024 open problem posed by Kornowski and Shamir regarding the optimal anytime convergence rates of GD.

</details>


### [164] [Invariance Entropy in the Dust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02279)
*Senhan Yao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过单一构造否定了Kawan关于控制系统不变熵的两个自然推广问题（已开放超过15年）：有限严格不变熵与普通不变熵不必相等，且严格不变熵在初始集的Hausdorff扰动下不必下半连续。该构造利用Cantor坐标存储无限符号指令、指数收缩坐标隐藏晚期不匹配、以及紧致匹配图强制精确符号一致，揭示了信息复杂性的新来源——并非由动力扩张产生，而是由薄不变几何下的精确可行约束持续性以及不变熵中的极限顺序所导致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 否定Kawan关于控制系统不变熵的两个长期未决的推广问题（已开放超过15年），即有限严格不变熵是否等于普通不变熵，以及严格不变熵在Hausdorff扰动下是否下半连续。

Method: 通过单一构造实现：利用Cantor坐标存储无限符号指令（编码信息）、指数收缩坐标使晚期不匹配在几何上不可见、以及紧致匹配图强制精确符号一致，从而构建一个连续时间控制系统。

Result: 证明了有限严格不变熵不必与普通不变熵相等，且严格不变熵不必在初始集的Hausdorff扰动下下半连续。

Conclusion: 识别了信息复杂性的新来源，它并非由动力扩张产生，而是由薄不变几何下的精确可行约束持续性以及不变熵中的极限顺序所导致。

Abstract: We answer negatively two natural general forms of Kawan's questions on invariance entropy for control systems, open for more than fifteen years, by a single construction. We show that finite strict invariance entropy need not coincide with ordinary invariance entropy, and that strict invariance entropy need not be lower semicontinuous under Hausdorff perturbations of the initial set. The construction is a continuous-time control system in which a Cantor coordinate stores an infinite symbolic instruction, an exponentially contracting coordinate makes late mismatches geometrically invisible, and a compact matching graph forces exact symbolic agreement. It identifies a source of information complexity not generated by dynamical expansion, but by the persistence of exact viability constraints under thin invariant geometry and by the order of limits in invariance entropy.

</details>


### [165] [Sensitivity Analysis and Robust Optimal Control for Coupled Evolution Inclusions with State-Dependent Maximal Monotone Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02339)
*Jinsheng Du,Boris Mordukhovich,Shengda Zeng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类由半线性发展包含和状态依赖极大单调算子控制的微分包含构成的强耦合非光滑系统，证明了参数化解映射的良定性、紧性及Painlevé-Kuratowski连续性，建立了Bolza型优化问题的最优对存在性、值函数连续性和最优解映射上半连续性，针对参数不确定性提出了固定参数最优控制、同时控制-参数设计、min-max鲁棒控制和Hurwicz型折中控制四种框架并证明了存在性，最后通过扫掠型系统的数值实验验证了理论预测的敏感性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对强耦合非光滑系统中状态依赖极大单调算子与半线性发展包含耦合系统的系统性优化分析，特别是在参数不确定性下的多种优化控制框架的统一处理。

Method: 采用变分分析和集值分析工具，先建立参数化解映射的良定性、紧性与连续性等基础性质，再分别针对Bolza型优化、固定参数最优控制、同时控制-参数设计、min-max鲁棒控制和Hurwicz型折中控制等不同优化框架进行理论分析，并辅以数值实验验证。

Result: 证明了Bolza型优化问题最优对的存在性、值函数的连续性以及最优解映射的上半连续性；建立了四种参数不确定性下的控制框架的存在性结果；数值实验验证了理论预测的敏感性与鲁棒性现象。

Conclusion: 本文为强耦合非光滑系统在参数不确定性下的优化与控制提供了完整的理论框架和数值验证，所建立的多种控制框架具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: We consider a class of strongly coupled nonsmooth systems consisting of a semilinear evolution inclusion and a differential inclusion governed by state-dependent maximal monotone operators. Our main contributions are fourfold. First, we collect the well-posedness, compactness, and Painlevé--Kuratowski continuity properties of the parameterized solution map required for the subsequent optimization analysis. Second, for Bolza-type optimization over the solution set, we prove the existence of optimal pairs, establish continuity properties of the value function, and derive upper semicontinuity of the optimal-solution map. Third, we study fixed-parameter optimal control, simultaneous control-parameter design, min--max robust control, and Hurwicz-type compromise control under parameter uncertainty, and we establish existence results for each formulation. Fourth, we report numerical experiments for sweeping-type systems that illustrate the sensitivity and robustness phenomena predicted by the theory.

</details>


### [166] [Boundary-Aware Quantization: Finite-Scale Decision Geometry of Neural Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01478)
*O. M. Kiselev*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统量化了量化对决策边界的影响，提出了多种边界变化度量指标（如局部对数几率边缘半径、边界Jaccard距离、低边缘边界带翻转等），并通过校准边界Jaccard预测留出边界Jaccard（r=0.947-0.994）。实验表明，8位权重量化可保留数字基准的所有测试标签，而边界感知的停止策略可显著降低边界翻转率。此外，固定比特边界间隙舍入项在4位时降低了边界Jaccard（0.457→0.435），但存在精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化是部署深度神经网络的关键技术，但量化引起的决策边界变化缺乏系统性的度量与分析。现有研究多关注量化后的精度损失，忽略了决策边界几何结构的改变，而边界变化直接影响模型的可靠性与可解释性。本文旨在建立一套全面的量化边界变化度量体系，并探索边界感知的量化优化策略。

Method: 提出了多种量化诱导的决策边界变化度量指标：局部对数几率边缘半径（local logit-margin radii）、一阶边界位移（first-order boundary displacement）、法向变化（normal variation）、切片边界Jaccard距离（slice-boundary Jaccard distance）、网格预测变化（grid prediction changes）、多类交界点计数（multiclass junction counts）以及低边缘边界带翻转（low-margin boundary-band flips）。在数字基准、MNIST、Fashion-MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上，对PTQ-W（权重量化）和优化舍入变体进行了实验。引入了边界感知的校准-测试停止规则，以及固定比特边界间隙舍入项（fixed-bit boundary-gap rounding term）。

Result: 8位权重量化保留了数字基准的所有测试标签，边界掩码Jaccard为0.428；4位时精度0.9733，边界Jaccard升至0.970，中位局部边界位移0.0290。校准-测试停止将数字基准的留出翻转率从0.0094降至0.0022，边界Jaccard从0.825降至0.524。在CIFAR-10上，边界感知停止在8位时实现了0.0083的翻转率和0.048的边界Jaccard。6位PTQ-W精度损失0.0029，改变了5.3%的留出决策和24.5%的低边缘边界带决策。边界间隙舍入项在4位时将边界Jaccard从0.457降至0.435，边界带对序翻转从0.3600降至0.3558。校准边界Jaccard与留出边界Jaccard的相关性为r=0.947-0.994。

Conclusion: 本文提出的量化边界变化度量体系有效刻画了量化对决策边界的影响，边界Jaccard可作为量化后模型可靠性的有效预测指标（高相关性）。边界感知的停止策略和边界间隙舍入项能够在不显著牺牲精度的情况下降低边界扰动。该工作为量化感知训练和量化后模型评估提供了新的几何视角，尤其适用于安全关键应用场景中对模型边界稳定性的需求。

Abstract: We measured quantization-induced decision-boundary changes using local logit-margin radii, first-order boundary displacement, normal variation, slice-boundary Jaccard distance, grid prediction changes, multiclass junction counts, and low-margin boundary-band flips. On the digits benchmark, 8-bit weight quantization preserved all test labels while producing boundary-mask Jaccard \(0.428\) on the PCA slice; at 4 bits, accuracy remained \(0.9733\), while boundary Jaccard rose to \(0.970\) and median local boundary shift reached \(0.0290\). Interpolation between adjacent quantization levels localized the visible reconfigurations at multiclass junctions, with 12, 34, and 17 triple-junction cells in the selected transitions. Calibration-to-test stopping reduced the digits held-out flip rate from \(0.0094\) to \(0.0022\) and boundary Jaccard from \(0.825\) to \(0.524\); the same stopping rule also reduced flips on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST. On official CIFAR-10 subsets, PTQ-W selected by accuracy gave 6-bit flip \(0.0367\) and boundary Jaccard \(0.184\), whereas boundary-aware stopping selected 8-bit flip \(0.0083\) and boundary Jaccard \(0.048\). On full CIFAR-10 with three seeds, 6-bit PTQ-W lost \(0.0029\) accuracy relative to float, changed \(5.3\%\) of held-out decisions, and changed \(24.5\%\) of low-margin boundary-band decisions. A fixed-bit boundary-gap rounding term changed the trade-off at 4 bits by reducing boundary Jaccard from \(0.457\) to \(0.435\) and boundary-band pair-order flip from \(0.3600\) to \(0.3558\), with an accuracy trade-off; the 3-bit stress test exposed the tuning limit of this surrogate. Calibration boundary Jaccard predicted held-out boundary Jaccard across PTQ-W and optimized rounding variants with \(r=0.947\)--\(0.994\).

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [PACE: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Plausible and Actionable Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01306)
*Pavel Iakovets,Liyanapathiranage Sudeepika Wajirakumari Samarathunga,Martin Thomas Horsch,Fadi Al Machot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE，一种模块化神经符号框架，通过将神经网络预测与符号推理（ASP规则）分离，生成符合领域知识和干预约束的可执行反事实解释，在Adult Income数据集上验证了可行性与有效性之间的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法虽能生成改变预测结果的输入变化，但缺乏显式机制融入领域知识和干预约束，导致推荐往往不现实或不可行。

Method: 提出PACE框架，采用模块化神经符号架构：神经网络模型负责分类预测，符号推理层（基于Answer Set Programming）负责在反事实生成过程中施加领域特定的可行性约束，两者分离且模型无关。

Result: 在Adult Income数据集上的实验表明，符号约束能够生成更符合领域可行性要求的解释，同时揭示了反事实有效性与合理性之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法能够有效提升反事实解释的可行性感知能力，为可解释AI中透明、可行的决策支持提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations explain machine learning predictions by identifying minimal input changes that would alter a model's decision. Although many existing methods successfully generate prediction-changing alternatives, they often produce unrealistic or infeasible recommendations due to a lack of explicit mechanisms for incorporating domain knowledge and intervention constraints. Neuro-symbolic AI offers a promising direction by combining data-driven predictive models with symbolic reasoning capable of representing human-understandable rules and feasible actions. This paper presents PACE, a modular neuro-symbolic framework for generating feasibility-aware counterfactual explanations. The framework separates prediction and reasoning into two components: a neural predictive model for classification and a symbolic reasoning layer that enforces domain-specific constraints during counterfactual generation. By explicitly modeling feasible interventions, the framework produces explanations consistent with domain knowledge while remaining interpretable and actionable. The approach is model-agnostic and adaptable to domains requiring realistic decision support. A case study is conducted on the Adult Income dataset, combining a multilayer perceptron classifier with Answer Set Programming (ASP) rules encoding feasible modifications to education, occupation, and working hours while preserving immutable attributes. Results highlight the trade-off between counterfactual validity and plausibility and show that symbolic constraints yield explanations that better satisfy domain-specific feasibility requirements, illustrating the potential of neuro-symbolic methods for transparent, feasibility-aware counterfactual explanation in explainable AI.

</details>


### [168] [Auto-FL-Research: Agentic Search for Federated Learning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01366)
*Holger R. Roth,Ziyue Xu,Chester Chen,Daguang Xu,Peter Cnudde,Andrew Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Auto-FL-Research (AFR)，一个用于联邦学习算法配方搜索的约束编码智能体工作流，在五个FLamby医疗任务和六个LEAF数据集上验证，结果混合：部分增益来源于真正的FL机制改变，部分仅为固定表面调参效应或单次运行伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习研究涉及大量微小但关键的算法选择（优化器、聚合规则、本地训练方案等），这些选择人工探索成本高且难以公平比较，因为候选变化可能同时改变FL训练或评估路径。

Method: 提出AFR框架，采用约束编码智能体工作流，智能体可提出并实现候选训练算法（聚合规则、客户端更新调度、本地目标、模型变体），任务配置文件固定变异表面、计算预算、通信协议和最终模型评估。每个campaign记录候选分数、运行时、编辑文件、产物和失败状态。

Result: 在五个FLamby医疗跨机构FL任务和六个LEAF数据集上评估，五种子重复实验在四个FLamby任务和五个LEAF配置上取得增益，但也暴露了种子敏感和搜索选择的失败案例。同预算控制表明部分增益对应FL配方改变，其他改进被固定表面标量控制复现或在重复/保留评估下失败。

Conclusion: 混合结果本身是贡献：展示了如何将智能体生成的候选方案区分为重复的FL机制、固定表面调参效应和选择的单次运行伪影三类，强调了自动化FL搜索中严格分离不同来源增益的重要性。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) research often depends on many small but consequential algorithmic choices: optimizer variants, server aggregation rules, local training schedules, normalization, regularization, and model architecture. These choices are expensive to explore manually and difficult to compare fairly when candidate changes can also alter the FL training or evaluation path. In this work, we present Auto-FL-Research (AFR), a constrained coding-agent workflow for FL algorithmic recipe search. Agents may propose and implement candidate training algorithms, including server aggregation rules, client update schedules, local objectives, and registered model variants, while task profiles fix the mutation surface, compute budget, communication contract, and final model evaluation. Each campaign records candidate scores, runtime, edited files, artifacts, and failure status. We evaluate AFR on five healthcare cross-silo FLamby tasks and on grouped-client profiles for the five fixed LEAF datasets plus the LEAF synthetic task. Five-seed repeat evaluations support gains on four FLamby tasks and five of six LEAF profiles, while also exposing seed-sensitive and search-selected failure cases. Same-budget controls show that several gains correspond to FL-recipe changes, whereas other improvements are recovered by fixed-surface scalar controls or fail under repeat or held-out evaluation. These mixed outcomes are part of the contribution: they show how agent-generated candidates can be separated into repeated FL mechanisms, fixed-surface tuning effects, and selected single-run artifacts.

</details>


### [169] [The Wiola Architecture for Efficient Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01394)
*Aryuemaan Kumar Chowdhury,Afreen Shaik,Yaparla Bhargavi,Brahma Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Wiola 是一个完全原创的小型语言模型（SLM）架构，提出了五种独立新颖的组件：螺旋旋转位置编码（SRPE）、门控跨层注意力（GCLA）、自适应令牌合并（ATM）、双流前馈网络（DSFF）和 WiolaRMSNorm，在多个规模上与 GPT-2、LLaMA-2、Mistral 进行了系统比较，并完全兼容 HuggingFace Transformers 生态系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型架构（如 GPT、LLaMA、Mistral、Falcon）在结构上具有高度同源性，缺乏从根本上重新设计的探索。作者旨在从第一性原理出发构建一个完全原创的 SLM 架构，以验证能否通过全新设计获得有竞争力的性能。

Method: 提出了五个独立新颖的组件：1) SRPE：在三维螺旋流形上嵌入位置，融合绝对、相对和层次位置信号；2) GCLA：每个解码器层通过软交叉注意力访问前两层的压缩摘要，增强层间连贯性；3) ATM：在中间网络层动态合并语义冗余的相邻令牌，降低注意力复杂度且不损失信息；4) DSFF：用两个并行流替代传统 MLP，通过逐维度可学习的门控融合；5) WiolaRMSNorm：在归一化中引入逐维度可学习的偏移向量，防止表示坍缩。

Result: 发布了四个规模（120M、360M、700M、1.5B 参数）的 Wiola 模型，提供了完整的数学推导、架构框图、复杂度分析，并与 GPT-2、LLaMA-2、Mistral 进行了系统比较。所有 22 个架构单元测试均通过，完全兼容 HuggingFace Transformers 生态系统。

Conclusion: Wiola 证明了从第一性原理重新设计 SLM 架构是可行且有竞争力的，五个独立新颖的组件均被验证有效，且模型具有良好的可扩展性和生态兼容性。

Abstract: We present Wiola, a fully original Small Language Model (SLM) architecture built from first principles, sharing no structural lineage with any existing model family including GPT, LLaMA, Mistral, or Falcon. Wiola introduces five independently novel components: (i) Spiral Rotary Positional Encoding (SRPE), which embeds token positions on a three-dimensional helical manifold combining absolute, relative, and hierarchical positional signals; (ii) Gated Cross-Layer Attention (GCLA), providing each decoder layer with soft cross-attention access to compressed summaries of two preceding layers for inter-layer coherence; (iii) Adaptive Token Merging (ATM), which dynamically merges se mantically redundant adjacent tokens in middle network layers to reduce attention complexity without information loss; (iv) Dual Stream Feed-Forward (DSFF), replacing the conventional MLP with two parallel streams fused by a learned per-dimension gate; and (v) WiolaRMSNorm, a modified normalisation introducing a per-dimension learned offset vector that prevents representation collapse. We provide complete mathematical derivations, architectural block diagrams, complexity analyses, and systematic comparisons against GPT-2, LLaMA-2, and Mistral. Wiola is released in four sizes (120M, 360M, 700M, and 1.5B parameters) and is fully compatible with the HuggingFace Transformers ecosystem, with all 22 architectural unit tests passing.

</details>


### [170] [Agent4cs: A Multi-agent System for Code Summarization in Large Hierarchical Codebases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01425)
*Yongjian Tang,Ezgi Sarikayak,Doruk Tuncel,Jie M. Zhang,Thomas Runkler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Agent4cs，一个多智能体框架，通过自底向上的方式对大型代码仓库进行摘要总结，包含摘要生成、关键词提取和质量保证三个智能体，在7个前沿模型上平均提升语义一致性8%，关键词覆盖率提升最高达38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码摘要方法多依赖单一语言模型，将源代码视为扁平文本，未能充分利用代码仓库中丰富的相互依赖关系和层次结构信息，难以理解大型、复杂、结构模糊且文档不完整的代码库。

Method: 提出多智能体框架Agent4cs，采用自底向上的摘要策略：摘要生成智能体负责生成鲁棒性摘要；关键词提取智能体主动识别子文件夹中的关键信息；质量保证智能体迭代优化输出的可读性、连贯性和完整性。

Result: 在7个前沿模型上，与两种结构化提示基线相比，Agent4cs在所有文件夹层级上平均提升语义一致性8%；在真实世界数据集上，归一化关键词覆盖率最高提升38%。

Conclusion: 多智能体协作的自底向上方法能有效利用代码仓库的层次结构信息，优于传统扁平文本处理的代码摘要方法。

Abstract: Understanding large, complex codebases, especially those with obfuscated structures and incomplete documentation, remains a significant challenge. Existing code summarization solutions often rely on a single language model or coding assistant like Claude Code, and treat source code as flat text, underutilizing the rich interdependencies and hierarchical information within a repository. To address these shortcomings, we propose Agent4cs - a multi-agent framework that summarizes large codebases in a bottom-up fashion, where a summarization agent focuses on producing robust summaries; a keyword-extraction agent proactively identifies critical information from subfolders; and a quality-assurance agent iteratively refines the outputs for readability, coherence, and completeness. Evaluated on 7 frontier models, Agent4cs improves semantic consistency across all folder levels by average 8% compared to two structured prompting baselines with code segments. Furthermore, extensive evaluation on real-world datasets demonstrates up to 38% gains in normalized keyword coverage rate over the same baselines.

</details>


### [171] [When Should Service Agents Reconsider? Difficulty-Routed Control in Customer-Service Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01426)
*Qian Chen,Chengyuan Liu,Xin Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种难度路由的服务控制架构，通过轻量级路由器将常规会话留在低成本基线路径，将操作耦合的会话路由到升级工作流，后者利用冲突感知通信和写入触发重新考虑机制，在关键后端写入前集中审核与保护，避免对所有会话统一施加额外控制。在零售和航空任务上验证了该方法能有效提升有操作冲突的服务请求的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主客服代理从对话界面转向操作执行角色（如退款、取消、改单等），面临服务控制问题：既要保持常规服务快速低摩擦，又要防止因客户指令、策略约束、企业记录与后端写入交互而导致的操作错误。

Method: 提出难度路由的服务控制架构：轻量级路由器区分常规会话与操作耦合会话；常规会话走低成本基线路径；操作耦合会话进入升级工作流，采用冲突感知通信和写入触发重新考虑机制，在关键后端写入前集中审核与保护。

Result: 在零售任务上，该方法一致提升了有操作冲突的服务请求的可靠性；路由证据表明更强控制定向作用于冲突请求而非泛化应用于常规会话；对话和工具使用分析显示增益来自证据收集、写入分离和写入前重新考虑，而非无差别的交互扩展或更宽的工具链；案例级证据显示升级工作流保留了备用计划、将检索记录绑定到正确动作、排序写入并分解多实体请求。航空任务验证了相同的服务控制逻辑。

Conclusion: 难度路由的服务控制架构能有效区分常规与冲突会话，在关键操作前集中审核，避免对所有会话施加统一控制，从而在保持效率的同时提升可靠性。

Abstract: Autonomous customer-service agents are shifting from conversational interfaces toward operational execution roles: they retrieve firm records, apply service policies, and execute backend writes such as refunds, cancellations, exchanges, order modifications, and reservation changes. This shift creates a service-control problem: firms must keep routine service fast and low-friction while preventing operational errors on requests where customer instructions, policy constraints, firm records, and backend writes interact. We propose a difficulty-routed service-control architecture that asks when service agents should reconsider before acting. A lightweight router keeps routine sessions on a low-cost baseline path and routes operationally coupled sessions to an escalated workflow. The escalated path uses conflict-aware communication and write-triggered reconsideration to concentrate deliberation and safeguards before consequential backend writes, rather than applying additional control uniformly across all service sessions. We evaluate the architecture on human-verified retail and airline tasks from $τ^{2}$-bench. In retail, the method improves reliability consistently on service requests with operational conflict. Routing evidence shows that stronger control is directed toward conflicted requests rather than broadly applied to routine ones. Dialogue and tool-use profiles suggest that gains do not come from indiscriminate interaction expansion or broader tool chains; instead, added turns and tool calls support evidence gathering, write separation, and pre-write reconsideration. Case-level evidence shows that the escalated workflow preserves fallback plans, binds retrieved records to the correct action, sequences writes, and decomposes multi-entity requests. Airline results extend the same service-control logic to reservation operations.

</details>


### [172] [CreativityNeuro: Steering Language Model Weights to Improve Divergent Thinking and Reduce Mode Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01433)
*Samuel Schapiro,Core Francisco Park,Felix Sosa,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需数据、无需重新训练的无梯度方法CreativityNeuro，通过对比权重引导增强大语言模型的发散性思维，在多项创造力评估中显著提升原创性、惊喜度和创造力，并有效减少模式崩溃，且能泛化到未见过的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在开放式问题上倾向于生成相似回答（人工蜂群效应），缺乏发散性思维能力，现有方法需要行为数据、重新训练或梯度微调，成本高昂。

Method: 提出CreativityNeuro，一种基于对比权重引导（contrastive weight steering）的数据无关方法，通过修改模型权重而非激活值来增强发散性思维，无需行为数据、重新训练或梯度微调。

Result: 在DAT任务上提升最多14个人类百分位点；在AUT和Task Task的大规模人工评估（N=720）中，在原创性、惊喜度和创造力上均取得显著提升；在所有三项任务中均可测地减少了模式崩溃；激活引导在DAT上表现相当但无法泛化到AUT和Task Task，而权重引导能有效泛化。

Conclusion: CreativityNeuro无需额外数据或复杂训练即可有效提升LLM在创造性领域的表现，提供了一种简单直接的增强方案。

Abstract: Divergent thinking is a crucial aspect of creativity, yet large language models (LLMs) tend to consistently generate similar responses to open-ended questions, in what has been termed the artificial hivemind effect. Here, we introduce CreativityNeuro, a data-free method for enhancing divergent thinking in LLMs via contrastive weight steering. We evaluate our method across multiple creativity assessments and report several main findings. On the Divergent Association Task (DAT), a vocabulary-space creativity test, CreativityNeuro improves performance by up to 14 human percentile points. Next, in a large-scale human evaluation (N=720) on the Alternative Uses Test (AUT) and the Task Task, CreativityNeuro achieves significant improvements in originality, surprise, and creativity, transferring to longer-form and more open-ended tasks. Importantly, we find that across all three tasks, CreativityNeuro demonstrably reduces measures of mode collapse. Moreover, activation steering achieves comparable performance to CreativityNeuro on the DAT, but it does not transfer to the AUT and Task Task, demonstrating the effectiveness of weight-space steering in generalizing to unseen tasks. In conclusion, CreativityNeuro improves divergent thinking and reduces mode collapse without requiring behavioral data, re-training, or gradient-based fine-tuning, providing a straightforward way to enhance LLM performance in creative domains.

</details>


### [173] [Discrete Diffusion Language Models for Interactive Radiology Report Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01436)
*Max Van Puyvelde,Halil Ibrahim Gulluk,Wim Van Criekinge,Olivier Gevaert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明了扩散语言模型（DiffusionGemma-26B）在医学视觉问答任务上可与同规模自回归模型（Gemma-4-26B）匹敌甚至超越，同时解码速度快3.5-4.4倍，并具备自回归模型缺乏的任意顺序填充能力，更适合临床报告场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学基础模型目前几乎全部采用自回归架构，而扩散语言模型在文本生成任务上已展现出竞争力，但在医学领域尚未被充分探索。本文旨在验证扩散模型在医学视觉问答中的有效性，并挖掘其独特优势（如任意顺序填充）在临床报告场景中的价值。

Method: 将混合专家扩散语言模型DiffusionGemma-26B与同尺寸自回归模型Gemma-4-26B在医学视觉问答数据集上进行对比，两者采用相同的LoRA微调方案，使用对冗长程度鲁棒的LLM裁判进行评分。测量解码速度并评估扩散模型特有的任意顺序填充能力。

Result: 扩散模型在所有医学视觉问答数据集上匹配或超越自回归模型；微调后的模型（3.8B活跃参数）与前沿视觉语言模型竞争力相当；解码速度提升3.5-4.4倍；扩散模型具备自回归模型无法比拟的任意顺序填充能力，可在放射科医生修复报告片段后填充中间文本。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型在医学领域不仅实现了与自回归模型相当的性能和更快的解码速度，还提供了对临床报告（常存在表述简略或不一致问题）更为自然的任意顺序填充能力，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Diffusion language models, which generate text by denoising a token canvas bidirectionally instead of emitting tokens left to right, have become competitive with autoregressive (AR) generation. Medical foundation models, however, remain almost entirely autoregressive. We adapt a mixture-of-experts diffusion language model, DiffusionGemma-26B, and benchmark it against its same-size AR sibling Gemma-4-26B under an identical LoRA recipe on medical visual question answering datasets, scored by a verbosity-robust LLM judge. Diffusion matches or exceeds AR on all of them, and the finetuned model (3.8B active) is competitive with frontier vision-language models; its decoding is also 3.5-4.4x faster. Beyond this parity, the diffusion model offers a drafting capability AR lacks: any-order infill. Because the canvas is denoised bidirectionally, a radiologist can fix report fragments and have the model fill the text between them, an operation inherent to diffusion but not to autoregression, which is subpar at it. This suits real reports, which are often terse or inconsistent across clinicians and institutions.

</details>


### [174] [Beyond Next-Token Prediction: An RLVR Proof of Concept for Tool-Use Agents on Atlassian Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01465)
*Karthikeya Aditya Vissa,Sankalp Mane,Ananya Mantravadi,Harshit Rajgarhia,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索了直接使用强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）在目标环境中针对小众企业SaaS工作流（如Jira/Confluence API）训练小型语言模型，通过合成环境证明该方法能从基线0.35-0.92提升奖励至0.95-1.00，但存在奖励手工设计不可扩展和部分场景奖励饱和两个主要局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通过下一个token预测训练，与在企业SaaS API中正确调用端点、嵌套参数和执行顺序的目标存在错配，导致静默失败（遗漏必填字段、幻觉工具、单次读取后停止）。

Method: 构建五个模拟Jira REST v3和Confluence v2 API的合成环境，使用基于工具调用轨迹的完全可验证奖励（无需实时API、无需学习型裁判、无需人工标注），对Qwen3-1.7B和Qwen3.5-4B进行GRPO训练，奖励由与训练相同的检查器计算。

Result: 在四个非退化奖励场景中，RL训练策略将平均奖励从4B基线0.35-0.92提升至0.95-1.00，最大增益出现在Confluence页面创建场景（0.35→1.00）。一个场景（ticket-transition）奖励饱和，4B基线已满分。

Conclusion: RLVR在小众企业API场景下能显著提升小型模型的工具调用成功率，是迈向面向结果优化的小模型的初步步骤；但手工构造可验证奖励无法规模化，且部分场景奖励函数设计需改进。

Abstract: Large language models are trained to predict the next token, not to act inside a specific API. In niche enterprise SaaS workflows -- where success means hitting the right endpoint with the right nested arguments in the right order -- this objective mismatch shows up as silent failures: dropped required fields, hallucinated tools, or early stops after a single read. We ask whether Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), applied directly in the target environment, closes the gap. As a proof of concept we build a suite of five synthetic environments emulating the Jira REST v3 and Confluence v2 APIs at schema fidelity; rewards are computed entirely from the tool-call trace, with no live API, no learned judge, and no human label in the loop. Scoring prompted Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3.5-4B on the same checkers that drive GRPO training, we find that on the four scenarios whose rewards are non-degenerate the RL-trained policy lifts average reward from a 4B-baseline range of 0.35--0.92 to 0.95--1.00, with the largest single gain on Confluence page creation ($0.35 \rightarrow 1.00$). We position this as a preliminary step toward outcome-optimised small models for niche enterprise APIs, and foreground two limitations a workshop reader should weigh: hand-crafting verifiable rewards does not scale beyond the handful of endpoints reported here, and one of our five scenarios (ticket-transition) has a saturating reward shape that the prompted 4B already maxes out.

</details>


### [175] [World Feedback for Clinical Agents: Diagnosing RL in FHIR Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01470)
*Ananya Mantravadi,Harshit Rajgarhia,Prasanna Desikan,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计发现MedAgentBench v1/v2存在41.7%的静默完成上限导致RL无效，构建MAB-v3（508任务，8.9%上限）。训练Qwen3-8B揭示两个结构障碍：能力上限（10/20任务类型零基础性能）和格式知识障碍（3/20类型需精确临床代码）。纯RL达到18.2% pass@1，规则SFT达34.1%，差距完全归因于这些障碍。提出决策/格式知识/查找分类法预测RL可学习性，并建议SFT注入代码、RL学习条件逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床协议执行任务适合从世界反馈中进行RL，但需要可靠的反馈通道和足够的基础能力。现有基准存在上限问题，需要构建更好的基准并理解RL应用于此类任务的障碍。

Method: 审计MedAgentBench v1/v2发现静默完成上限；构建MAB-v3（508任务）；训练Qwen3-8B进行纯RL和规则SFT对比实验；提出决策/格式知识/查找分类法分析RL可学习性。

Result: 纯RL达到18.2% pass@1，规则SFT达34.1%，差距15.9个百分点。10/20任务类型零基础性能，3/20类型需精确代码不可探索发现。

Conclusion: 决策/格式知识/查找分类法预测RL可学习性；SFT注入代码知识，RL学习条件决策逻辑是有效策略。

Abstract: Clinical protocol-execution tasks -- checking a lab value, applying a threshold, placing a correctly structured FHIR order -- are natural candidates for RL from world feedback: once clinical SMEs encode decision logic into a verifier, that verifier grades unlimited rollouts without per-episode annotation. But applying RL requires a sound feedback channel and sufficient base capability. We audit MedAgentBench v1/v2, find a 41.7\% silent-finish ceiling that makes inaction the RL dominant strategy, and construct \textbf{MedAgentBench-v3 (MAB-v3)} (508 tasks, 8.9\% ceiling). Training Qwen3-8B exposes two structural barriers: a \emph{capability ceiling} (10/20 task types have 0\% base performance, zero gradient) and a \emph{format-knowledge barrier} (3/20 types require exact clinical codes undiscoverable by exploration). Pure RL reaches 18.2\% pass@1 vs.\ 34.1\% for rule-based SFT; the 15.9~pp gap is attributable entirely to these barriers. A decision/format-knowledge/lookup taxonomy predicts RL learnability and prescribes the fix: SFT to inject codes, RL to learn conditionals.

</details>


### [176] [Procedural Memory Distillation: Online Reflection for Self-Improving Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01480)
*Ye Liu,Srijan Bansal,Bo Pang,Yang Li,Zeyu Leo Liu,Yifei Ming,Zixuan Ke,Shafiq Joty,Semih Yavuz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出程序性记忆蒸馏（PMD），通过跨回合收集轨迹、策略反思和高级行为模式作为记忆，并用记忆条件自教师蒸馏策略，使模型逐步内化程序性知识。在Qwen3-8B和OLMo3-Instruct-7B上，PMD相比SDPO在SCIKNOWEVAL提升3.8-5.5%，在LIVECODEBENCH提升7.9-13.6%，且共演化设计是关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR和SDPO方法每个回合仅使用回合级验证信号更新策略，忽略了rollout中的丰富程序性信息，且跨回合/跨epoch的经验信号（如哪些策略持续通过验证、哪些失败模式持续存在）无法被局部更新捕捉。

Method: PMD构建三层抽象的程序性记忆：原始轨迹、自反思策略与经验教训、跨问题的行为模式。这些记忆在线地从模型自身轨迹中提取，并由记忆条件自教师利用累积经验对学生模型的rollout进行监督，使学生逐步将程序性知识内化到参数中。核心设计原则是共演化：策略生成轨迹更新记忆，记忆塑造监督信号更新策略。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B和OLMo3-Instruct-7B上，PMD相比SDPO在SCIKNOWEVAL提升3.8-5.5%，在LIVECODEBENCH提升7.9-13.6%。消融实验显示，冻结记忆或策略均导致性能下降超过10%。

Conclusion: PMD通过跨回合程序性记忆的蒸馏有效提升了RLVR训练的样本效率和策略质量，共演化机制是性能提升的核心驱动力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), along with recent selfdistillation variants such as SDPO, evaluates each rollout against a verifier and updates the policy from that episode-level signal. However, the richer procedural information in the rollout is rarely retained or reused. Across episodes and epochs, the model repeatedly encounters related problems under a changing policy, producing cross-episode signals that episode-local updates cannot capture: which strategies consistently pass verification, which failure modes persist, which patterns recur. We propose Procedural Memory Distillation (PMD), which converts these crossepisode signals into reusable procedural memory and distills it into the policy's weights during training. This memory functions as a training scaffold, absorbed into the policy itself, yielding a memory-free model at inference. PMD organizes the memory at three levels of abstraction: raw trajectories, self-reflected strategies and lessons, and higher-level behavioral patterns that recur across problems, all extracted online from the model's own trajectories. A memory-conditioned self-teacher draws on the accumulated experience to supervise the student on its own rollouts, enabling student to progressively internalize procedural knowledge within its parameters. The central design principle is co-evolution: the policy generates rollouts that update the memory, and memory shapes the supervision that updates the policy. Empirically, across Qwen3-8B and OLMo3-Instruct-7B, PMD improves over SDPO by 3.8-5.5% on SCIKNOWEVAL and 7.9-13.6% on LIVECODEBENCH. Co-evolution powers these gains: freezing either the memory or the policy trails PMD by more than 10% across SCIKNOWEVAL domains.

</details>


### [177] [The Agentic Garden of Forking Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01507)
*Jiacheng Miao,Jonathan K Pritchard,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现AI智能体能够复现人类研究者在数据分析中的主观变异性，并提出了m值（多宇宙值）和Agentic Bootstrap方法，用于量化单一分析结果在合理分析空间中的极端程度，从而评估科学证据的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实证研究很少存在唯一正确的分析方法，不同的分析选择（分叉路径）可能从同一数据得出不同结论，但这些隐藏的路径难以被观察和量化。作者希望使这些分析路径显式化，并解决选择性探索与报告这一长期存在的问题。

Method: 1）使用AI智能体并赋予不同人格（persona）来模拟不同人类研究者的分析选择；2）在四个高风险领域（包括移民数据分析）进行实验，对比AI与人类研究者的分析结果；3）提出m值（多宇宙值），衡量某一分析结果在合理分析空间中的极端程度；4）提出Agentic Bootstrap方法，通过AI智能体对合理分析路径进行采样，估计m值。

Result: 1）AI智能体能够复现人类研究者72%的意识形态差异导致的效果估计差距；2）86%的AI分析通过独立AI审查，78%通过多数人类专家审查，表明从最终报告难以识别明显问题；3）在人类移民数据研究中，13.5%的人类报告分析落在分析空间中最极端的5%区域内（m<0.05）。

Conclusion: 科学证据的评估不应仅基于单一报告的分析结果，还应考虑其在所有合理分析结果分布中的位置。Agentic Bootstrap使这一分布可观测，并将其转化为科学可信度的判断标准。

Abstract: Empirical research rarely admits a unique analysis. Different analytical choices can lead to different conclusions from the same data, yet these hidden forking paths are difficult to observe. We show that AI agents capture much of the analytical variation among human researchers while making these paths explicit. Across four high-stakes domains, assigning different personas is sufficient for AI agents to report divergent, often opposing, conclusions from the same data and question, with findings systematically aligned with those beliefs. In a study in which 42 human research teams analyzed the same immigration dataset, AI agents reproduced 72% of the human ideological gap in reported effect estimates. Despite reaching opposing conclusions, it is difficult to identify clear issues in each analysis based on the final AI reports: 86% passed independent AI review and 78% passed majority human expert review. These findings suggest that the central challenge is often not flawed analyses, but selective exploration and reporting from a large space of methodologically defensible analyses. AI agents may amplify this longstanding problem by making such exploration inexpensive and scalable. To address this, we introduce the m-value (multiverse value), the probability that an analysis path would produce a claim at least as extreme as the reported one. We further introduce Agentic Bootstrap, which estimates the m-value by using AI agents to sample plausible analysis paths. Applied to the human immigration study, 13.5% of reported human analyses fell in the most extreme 5% of the analysis space (m<0.05). Scientific evidence should therefore be evaluated not only by a single reported analysis but also by its position within the distribution of analyses that could reasonably have been reported. Agentic Bootstrap makes this distribution observable and turns it into a criterion for scientific credibility.

</details>


### [178] [Janus: a Playground for User-Involved Agentic Permission Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01510)
*Natalie Grace Brigham,Eugene Bagdasarian,Tadayoshi Kohno,Franziska Roesner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Janus是一个用于实现和评估用户参与的代理权限管理设计的游乐场系统，包含Janus-Core（模块化代理系统）和Janus-Harness（自动评估框架）。通过实现六种权限助手并评估，发现用户输入对隐私安全至关重要、AI辅助可降低认知负担、需考虑用户权限疲劳等，且没有单一设计在所有上下文中表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主执行工具调用的AI代理引发了权限管理问题，但用户在此过程中的角色尚未得到充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究用户在代理权限管理中应扮演和能扮演的角色。

Method: 提出Janus系统，包含Janus-Core（模块化代理系统，支持多种权限管理设计）和Janus-Harness（自动评估框架）。基于概念模型识别用户参与的关键设计轴，实现六种权限助手，在三个场景和三个合成响应者上评估。

Result: 用户输入对增强隐私和安全至关重要；AI增强用户决策可降低认知负担；需考虑用户权限疲劳等现实行为；没有单一设计在所有上下文中表现最优。

Conclusion: 需要更原则性且上下文敏感的方法来部署代理系统中的权限助手。Janus已公开可用，支持未来在代理系统设计这一维度的研究。

Abstract: AI agents that autonomously execute tool calls on a user's behalf raise pressing questions about permission management: what role could users play, and what role should they play? Despite many proposed approaches, the user's role in agentic permission management remains under explored. We introduce Janus, a playground system for implementing and evaluating user-involved agentic permission management designs. Janus consists of two components: Janus-Core, a modular agentic system supporting a diverse spectrum of permission management designs, and Janus-Harness, an automated evaluation framework. Grounded in a conceptual model that identifies key design axes for user involvement, we implement six permission assistants spanning the design space and evaluate them across three scenarios and three synthetic responders. We demonstrate that user input is critical and can significantly strengthen privacy and security, that AI augmentation of user decisions can help reduce cognitive load, and that realistic user behavior including permission fatigue must be accounted for in system design. No single design performs optimally across all contexts, motivating a more principled and context-sensitive approach to deploying permission assistants in agentic systems. Janus is publicly available to support future investigation into this dimension of agentic system design.

</details>


### [179] [Revisiting Chain-of-Thought Reasoning under Limited Supervision: Semi-supervised Chain-of-Thought Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01511)
*Hongyang He,Jiuming Liu,Victor Sanchez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出半监督思维链学习框架Semi-CoT，利用未标注问题生成伪推理链，通过答案级语义熵选择低熵链作为可靠伪CoT演示。实验显示熵门控能筛选高精度伪CoT（91.36%-100%），但在不同数据集上效果不一，部分任务有增益，部分出现负迁移或达到上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT方法主要将推理链用作推理时的提示，很少将生成的推理轨迹作为半监督学习信号重复利用。本文旨在探索如何利用未标注问题构造伪推理监督信号，激活模型在半监督场景下的推理能力。

Method: Semi-CoT框架：对每个未标注问题采样多个伪CoT，计算答案级语义熵，选择低熵推理链作为可靠伪CoT演示，从而将CoT从推理时精炼扩展到半监督伪监督的自训练视角。

Result: 在AQuA、SVAMP、GSM8K、MultiArith上的实验表明：熵门控选择的高精度伪CoT的伪答案精度达91.36%-100%；Semi-CoT在SVAMP和GSM8K上有小幅提升，AQuA出现负迁移，MultiArith达到性能上限。

Conclusion: 未标注问题能提供可靠的伪推理信号，但其有效利用仍需更强的演示选择机制或学生训练策略。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has emerged as an effective approach for activating latent reasoning capabilities in large language models. However, most existing CoT methods use reasoning chains mainly as inference-time prompts, while the generated reasoning traces are rarely reused as semi-supervised learning signals. In this report, we define \textbf{Semi-supervised Chain-of-Thought Learning} and propose \textbf{Semi-CoT}, a simple framework that uses unlabeled questions to construct pseudo reasoning supervision. Semi-CoT samples multiple pseudo-CoTs for each unlabeled question, estimates answer-level semantic entropy, and selects low-entropy reasoning chains as reliable pseudo-CoT demonstrations. This extends the self-training view of CoT from inference-time refinement to semi-supervised pseudo-supervision. Pilot experiments on AQuA, SVAMP, GSM8K, and MultiArith show that the entropy gate selects high-precision pseudo-CoTs, with pseudo-answer precision ranging from $91.36\%$ to $100\%$. Semi-CoT also gives small gains on SVAMP and GSM8K, while AQuA shows negative transfer and MultiArith reaches a ceiling. These results suggest that unlabeled questions can provide reliable pseudo reasoning signals, but their effective use still requires stronger demonstration selection or student training.

</details>


### [180] [OPINE-World: Programmatic World Modeling with Ontology-error-Prioritized Interactive Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01531)
*David Courtis,Wenhao Li,Scott Sanner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OPINE-World 是一个结合了程序合成与世界模型学习的 LLM 智能体，通过两个合作代理（行动者与合成者）在线交互学习面向对象的程序化世界模型，并利用贝叶斯本体误差度量引导探索，在 ARC-AGI-3 基准上无需逐游戏训练即解决了 20/25 个游戏，动作效率达 78.4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型方法存在两难：深度网络模型数据效率低且分布外迁移差，而程序合成模型虽数据高效且可重用，但此前仅适用于具有给定对象词汇的结构化状态世界，无法扩展到需要灵活假设对象结构的像素渲染环境。

Method: OPINE-World 构建两个合作代理的假设-测试循环：一个在环境中行动，另一个通过代码合成世界模型并结合回放验证与基于模型的规划；采用贝叶斯本体误差（ontology error）度量来指导探索，自适应地确定对象类型和词汇。

Result: 在 ARC-AGI-3 基准上，OPINE-World 无需逐游戏训练即可解决 20/25 个游戏，动作效率得分 78.4（人类基线水平）。

Conclusion: 面向对象的程序化世界模型结合在线交互与探索引导策略，能够在技能获取效率基准上达到接近人类的性能，为数据效率与泛化能力之间的权衡提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Learning how an environment behaves from interaction is central to building agents that adapt to unfamiliar tasks. World models learned with deep networks are flexible but data-hungry and transfer poorly beyond their training distribution. Program-synthesized world models, written as source code by LLMs and refined through counterexample-guided inductive synthesis (CEGIS), are instead data-efficient and reusable, yet they have been demonstrated mainly on structured-state worlds with a given object vocabulary, and a single program search does not scale to pixel-rendered environments whose object structure must be hypothesized flexibly. We introduce OPINE-World, an LLM agent that learns an object-centric programmatic world model online from interaction. OPINE-World couples two cooperating agents in a loop of hypothesis and test, one acting in the environment and one synthesizing the model in code with replay verification and model-based planning, and it steers exploration with a Bayesian measure of object-type adequacy we call ontology error. We evaluate OPINE-World on ARC-AGI-3, a benchmark for skill-acquisition efficiency in which the object vocabulary, the goal, and the action semantics are withheld. OPINE-World solves 20 of 25 games without per-game training and reaches an action-efficiency score of 78.4 against the human baseline.

</details>


### [181] [Scaling Trends for Lie Detector Oversight in Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01567)
*Oskar J. Hollinsworth,Ann-Kathrin Dombrowski,Sam Adam-Day,Adam Gleave,Chris Cundy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将SOLiD方法扩展到更大规模模型（1B至405B参数），在更真实的偏好学习场景中验证其效果。研究发现：未检测出的欺骗率从34%（1B模型）降至14%（405B模型，检测器真阳性率99%），且可在微调阶段完全移除昂贵的人工标注者而不显著增加欺骗。但SOLiD对检测器训练数据与偏好训练数据之间的分布偏移敏感，可能导致假阳性率升至不可实际的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型中的欺骗行为难以监控和防范，需要高昂成本进行监督。SOLiD方法虽被提出用于通过谎言检测器识别需审查的响应，但其在更大模型和更现实场景中的表现尚不清楚，因此需要扩展评估。

Method: 将SOLiD方法扩展到1B至405B参数规模的大模型，在更多样化且现实的偏好学习环境中进行评估。通过控制检测器真阳性率（99%），测量不同规模模型下未检测出的欺骗率，并探索在微调阶段完全移除人工标注者的可行性。

Result: 1）模型规模越大，未检测出的欺骗率越低（从1B模型的34%降至405B模型的14%）；2）可在微调阶段完全移除人工标注者而不引起统计上显著的欺骗增加；3）SOLiD对训练数据分布偏移敏感，会导致假阳性率升至不可实际的水平。

Conclusion: SOLiD方法在较大模型上展现有利的扩展性，能够在不使用人工标注的情况下维持低欺骗率，但其实际应用受限于对分布偏移的敏感性，需要进一步解决该问题。

Abstract: Deceptive behavior in LLMs is costly to monitor and prevent, motivating approaches such as Scalable Oversight via Lie Detectors (SOLiD) (Cundy & Gleave, 2025), which uses lie detectors to identify responses for review by high-cost labelers. In this paper, we scale SOLiD to larger models and evaluate it in more diverse and realistic preference-learning settings. We find favorable scaling: undetected deception drops from 34% for 1B-parameter models to 14% for 405B-parameter models at a detector true positive rate of 99%, and expensive human labelers can be removed entirely from the fine-tuning phase without a statistically significant increase in deception. However, SOLiD is sensitive to distribution shift between detector training and preference-training data, which can drive detector false positive rates to impractical levels.

</details>


### [182] [EO-Agents: A Three-Agent LLM Pipeline for Earth Observation Hypothesis Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01584)
*Mahyar Ghazanfari,Amin Tabrizian,Armin Mehrabian,Peng Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于NASA地球观测知识图谱的假设生成pipeline，利用异构图表征历史共现关系，结合三智能体LLM流程生成结构化研究假设，在1,475个数据集上产出160个跨领域假设，并通过2×2×2因子实验揭示了LLM评估中评判者偏差的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大模型科学假设生成工作大多依赖非结构化文献和自由形式文本，缺乏在结构化知识图谱上直接进行假设生成的方法，尤其在地球观测领域。

Method: 1) 异构图表征神经网络基于历史共现关系对候选数据集配对进行排序；2) 三智能体LLM流水线（过滤、生成、评估）输出结构化研究假设；3) 在1,475个NASA数据集上验证；4) 采用2×2×2因子实验（GPT-5.2 vs Claude Sonnet 4.6）分析评估稳定性。

Result: 产出160个假设，涵盖生态水文学、冰川学、气溶胶-云相互作用、植被物候学、平流层化学等领域；模型预测的新颖数据集配对与文献中真实共现案例的合理性相当；2×2×2实验中假设排名稳定但绝对分数严重依赖评判者身份。

Conclusion: 该pipeline能够生成科学连贯但尚未被探索的数据集组合；单评判者LLM评估存在显著偏差，需谨慎解读绝对评分结果。

Abstract: Large language models have recently been explored for scientific hypothesis generation, but most prior work relies on unstructured literature and free-form textual claims. We present a pipeline for Earth observation that grounds hypothesis generation directly in the NASA Earth Observation Knowledge Graph. A heterogeneous graph neural network trained on historical co-usage relations ranks candidate dataset pairings, and a three-agent LLM pipeline filters, generates, and evaluates structured research hypotheses. Applied to 1,475 NASA datasets, the system produces 160 hypotheses spanning multiple Earth-science domains, including ecohydrology, glaciology, aerosol--cloud interactions, vegetation phenology, and stratospheric chemistry. Model-predicted novel dataset pairings are rated nearly as plausible as held-out real co-usages from the literature, indicating that the pipeline surfaces scientifically coherent yet unexplored combinations. A 2*2*2 factorial experiment across GPT-5.2 and Claude Sonnet 4.6 shows that hypothesis rankings remain stable, while absolute scores depend strongly on judge identity, highlighting limitations of single-judge LLM evaluation.

</details>


### [183] [Hawk: Harnessing Hardware-Aware Knowledge for High-Performance NPU Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01590)
*Junyi Wen,Ruiyan Zhuang,Yongjia Xu,Pengtu Li,Rui Zou,Hongyi Chen,Chingman Wan,Puxu Yang,Wuhui Chen,Yanlin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Hawk是一个无需训练的框架，通过三大核心模块（运行时知识合成、瓶颈感知知识检索、效果驱动知识蒸馏）将硬件感知知识注入LLM，使NPU内核生成准确率从49.4%提升至80.0%，执行速度最高提升2.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NPU高性能内核开发是行业瓶颈，开发者需手动处理隐式硬件约束和严格存储层次。现有LLM缺乏硬件先验知识，直接移植代码虽能通过编译但会引发运行时崩溃和性能下降。

Method: 提出Hawk框架，包含三个模块：(1)运行时知识合成模块，采用三重可执行知识表示将错误上下文与可执行语义耦合；(2)瓶颈感知知识检索模块，实现2D检索范式将查询投影到正交的句法空间和硬件对齐的语义空间；(3)效果驱动知识蒸馏模块，基于经验执行反馈利用LLM驱动的语义仲裁持续修剪错误和合并冗余。

Result: 在真实NPU负载上，Hawk将生成准确率从49.4%提升至80.0%，执行速度相比最先进基线最高提升2.2倍。

Conclusion: 通过硬件感知知识注入，Hawk有效解决了LLM在NPU内核生成中的不可靠问题，实现了高质量、高性能的自动化NPU内核开发。

Abstract: Developing high-performance kernels for Neural Processing Units (NPUs) is a critical industry bottleneck, requiring developers to manually navigate implicit hardware constraints and strict memory hierarchies. While large language models offer immense automation potential, they fail catastrophically on NPUs due to a fundamental lack of hardware-specific priors. Naively transplanting code snippets from similar NPU kernels may pass the compiler, but it consistently triggers runtime crashes and performance degradation by blindly violating underlying hardware constraints. To overcome this, we introduce Hawk, a training-free framework that harnesses hardware-aware knowledge through three core modules: (1) Run-Time Knowledge Synthesis Module, which employs a Triple-Part Executable Knowledge Representation to inherently couple the error context with executable semantics; (2) Bottleneck-Aware Knowledge Retrieval Module, which implements a 2D-Retrieval paradigm to project queries into orthogonal syntactic and hardware-aligned semantic spaces; and (3) Effect-Driven Knowledge Distillation Module, which leverages LLM-driven semantic arbitration to continuously distill the knowledge by pruning errors and consolidating redundancies based on the empirical execution feedback. Extensive evaluations on real-world NPU workloads demonstrate that Hawk elevates generation accuracy from 49.4% to 80.0%, while achieving up to a 2.2x execution speedup over state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [184] [Safe and Adaptive Cloud Healing: Verifying LLM-Generated Recovery Plans with a Neural-Symbolic World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01595)
*Junyan Tan,Haoran Lin,Siyuan Guo,Yichen Fang,Xinyue Luo,Tianyu Shen,Zeyu Qiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PASE提出了一种基于神经符号程序合成的故障自愈框架，将LLM作为规划引擎生成恢复计划，结合神经符号世界模型进行仿真验证，并通过DRL训练的元提示优化器引导LLM规划过程，在真实云故障数据集上实现恢复时间降低40%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法虽结合LLM的语义理解与DRL的策略优化，但采用顺序松耦合架构，未能充分利用LLM的生成和推理能力，导致故障恢复效率低下。

Method: 将故障恢复重构为神经符号程序合成任务，包含三个核心组件：LLM作为规划合成引擎生成结构化恢复计划；神经符号世界模型通过仿真验证计划可行性；基于DRL训练的元提示优化器学习生成最优提示以引导LLM规划过程，形成"推理-规划-验证-适配"的紧耦合闭环。

Result: 在真实云故障注入数据集上，PASE将平均系统恢复时间降低40%以上，并在未知故障场景下提升了故障检测精度。

Conclusion: PASE通过统一LLM推理、模型辅助验证和元学习引导，推动了自主系统管理的发展，实现了超越预定义动作空间的动态、上下文感知恢复策略生成。

Abstract: As the scale and complexity of cloud-based AI systems continue to escalate, ensuring service reliability through rapid fault detection and adaptive recovery has become a critical challenge. While existing approaches integrate Large Language Models (LLMs) for semantic understanding and Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) for policy optimization, they often rely on sequential, loosely coupled architectures that underutilize the generative and reasoning capabilities of LLMs. In this paper, we propose a paradigm shift with PASE, a Planning-Aware Semantic self-healing engine, a novel fault self-healing framework that reconceptualizes recovery as a neuro-symbolic program synthesis task. PASE employs an LLM as a core Plan Synthesis Engine to generate structured recovery plans from a library of semantic primitives. A Neural-Symbolic World Model verifies plan feasibility through simulation, while a Meta-Prompt Optimizer, trained via DRL, learns to generate optimal prompts that guide the LLM's planning process. This tight reason-plan-verify-adapt loop enables dynamic, context-aware recovery strategy generation beyond predefined action spaces. Experiments on a real-world cloud fault injection dataset demonstrate that PASE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods, reducing average system recovery time by over 40% and improving fault detection accuracy in unknown fault scenarios. Our framework advances autonomous system management by unifying LLM-based reasoning with model-assisted verification and meta-learned guidance.

</details>


### [185] [SemHash-LLM: A Multi-Granularity Semantic Hashing Framework for Document Deduplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01601)
*Xinyi Fang,Kejian Tong,Jiabei Liu,Tao Ning,Yuhang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SemHash LLM框架，通过多粒度语义哈希、注意力加权MinHash、对比边界学习和选择性LLM裁决，在保证语义等价性的同时实现大规模文档去重的高效性，神经验证成本低于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模文档去重需要在海量语料上保持语义等价性同时保证效率，现有方法在语义保留与计算成本之间存在矛盾。

Method: 采用多粒度统一框架，包括：(1)语义投影哈希在蒸馏LLM嵌入空间中学习紧凑二值编码；(2)注意力加权MinHash抑制模板噪声、突出信息内容；(3)对比边界学习与不确定性估计提升鲁棒性；(4)选择性LLM裁决进行最终验证；通过门控融合整合字符、词元和文档级信号，并采用级联过滤流水线进行候选缩减。

Result: 实现了强重复检测质量，神经验证成本低于1%。

Conclusion: 多粒度级联框架能够有效平衡语义去重的准确性与效率，选择性LLM裁决极大降低了计算开销。

Abstract: Large scale document deduplication must preserve semantic equivalence while remaining efficient over massive corpora. We present SemHash LLM, a multi granularity framework that unifies semantic projection hashing, attention weighted MinHash, contrastive boundary learning, and selective LLM based adjudication. The method combines character, token, and document level signals through gated fusion, then applies a cascaded filtering pipeline for efficient candidate reduction. Semantic projection hashing learns compact binary codes in distilled LLM embedding space, while attention weighted Min- Hash suppresses boilerplate and emphasizes informative content. Adaptive decision boundaries and uncertainty estimation further improve robustness across template pollution, short text perturbation, containment, and viral fragments. Experiments show that SemHash LLM achieves strong duplicate detection quality with less than one percent neural verification cost.

</details>


### [186] [Profit-Based Counterfactual Explanations for Product Improvement: A Case Study of Manga Sales in Japan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01610)
*Keita Kinjo,Takeshi Ebina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于利润最大化的反事实解释框架（PBCE），将距离项重新解释为产品属性修改成本，无需外生指定目标值，直接以利润为优化目标，解决了回归设置中目标有效性和距离度量可解释性不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法在回归场景中存在两个关键问题：一是需要外生指定目标输出值，缺乏有效性验证；二是距离度量的实际可解释性不足。此外，现有方法关注改变预测值而非优化决策目标，与实际管理营销中的利润最大化需求不匹配。

Method: 将反事实解释重新表述为利润最大化问题，利润作为主要优化目标，同时将距离项重新定义为修改产品属性的成本，从而构建 PBCE 框架，无需外生目标值。

Result: PBCE 消除了对外生目标指定的依赖，赋予距离项明确的经济学解释（即属性修改成本），并将反事实解释从预测改变导向决策目标优化。

Conclusion: PBCE 为管理营销领域的反事实解释提供了一种经济上可解释且与决策目标一致的解决方案。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanation (CE) is widely used to enhance the interpretability of machine learning models and support data-driven decision-making based on model predictions. However, existing CE methods typically require two exogenously specified inputs: a desired output value (target) and a distance function that quantifies changes in explanatory variables. In regression settings, neither the validity of target specification nor the practical interpretation of the distance metric has been sufficiently addressed. Furthermore, most existing CE methods focus on altering predictions rather than optimizing a decision objective, even though real-world decision-making often requires explicit objective maximization. To address these limitations, we formulate CE as a profit maximization problem in management and marketing contexts and propose a framework termed profit-based counterfactual explanation (PBCE). PBCE eliminates the need for exogenous target specification by directly maximizing profit as the primary optimization objective. Concurrently, the distance term is reinterpreted as the cost of modifying product attributes, providing a clear and economically grounded interpretation.

</details>


### [187] [Scaling with Confidence: Calibrating Confidence of LLMs for Adaptive Test Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01612)
*Xuqing Yang,Yi Yuan,Shanzhe Lei,Xuhong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出C3RL算法，通过将正确性、置信度校准和数据集参考准确性奖励整合到RL训练中，改善LLM的置信度校准；并基于校准后的置信度提出CAS自适应推理策略，在降低推理预算的同时提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL奖励设计仅关注响应正确性，忽略了对模型准确表达置信度的激励，导致模型在不确定时过度自信地产生幻觉，性能提升伴随校准不良。

Method: 1) C3RL：在RL中整合正确性奖励、置信度校准奖励和数据集参考准确性奖励。2) CAS：基于校准后的置信度，在推理时自适应分配计算资源。

Result: 在8个文本和多模态数据集上，C3RL在不牺牲准确性的前提下改善了校准，优于当前SOTA；CAS在域内和域外数据集上超越多数投票，推理预算最多降低12.33倍。

Conclusion: C3RL与CAS的协同为部署更可靠且资源高效的LLM铺平了道路。

Abstract: Training large language models (LLMs) with reinforcement learning (RL) has significantly advanced their performance on reasoning and question-answering tasks. However, prevailing RL reward designs typically prioritize response correctness, neglecting to incentivize models to express their confidence accurately. This leads to a critical problem: performance gains are often accompanied by poor calibration between confidence and accuracy, misleading models to overconfidently hallucinate when uncertain. To address this limitation, we propose $\textbf{C}$orrectness and $\textbf{C}$onfidence $\textbf{C}$alibration $\textbf{R}$einforcement $\textbf{L}$earning ($\textbf{C3RL}$), a novel RL algorithm integrating correctness, calibration and dataset-informed reference accuracy rewards together. Comprehensive evaluation across 8 text and multimodal datasets demonstrates that C3RL enhances calibration without sacrificing accuracy, outperforming the current state-of-the-art method in both performance and calibration metrics. Utilizing the well-calibrated verbalized confidence from C3RL, we further introduce $\textbf{C}$onfidence-based $\textbf{A}$daptive Test Time $\textbf{S}$caling ($\textbf{CAS}$), an adjustable inference-time strategy that allocates computational resources based on response confidence. Experiments show that CAS surpasses majority voting on both in-domain and out-of-domain datasets while reducing the inference budget by up to 12.33 times. We believe the synergy of C3RL and CAS paves the way for deploying more reliable and resource-efficient LLMs. The code, data and models will be released.

</details>
